    let advanced = output_json(f.command(&["clock", "advance", "120s", "--port", port]));
    assert_eq!(advanced["advancedMs"], 120_000);
    let after = host.auth_request("GET", "/effects", json!(null), &auth);
    let settled = host.auth_request("POST", &format!("/effects/{}/done", effect["id"].as_str().unwrap()), json!({"result":"sent once"}), &auth);
    println!("effect clock: after_advance={after} settlement={settled}");
    assert_eq!(settled["ok"], true, "{settled}");
    assert_eq!(after["effects"][0]["state"], "running", "{after}");
    assert_eq!(after["effects"][0]["leaseUntil"].as_i64().unwrap() - effect["leaseUntil"].as_i64().unwrap(), 120_000);
    assert_eq!(settled["effect"]["attempt"], 1);
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth)["effects"], json!([]));
    println!("effect clock: advanced_ms=120000 lease_ms=60000 requeues=0 attempts=1 wall_ms={}", start.elapsed().as_millis());
}

#[test]
fn clock_advance_preserves_a_live_action_claim_session_and_settlement() {
    let f = Fixture::new("action-live");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("use server\n{SOURCE}\naction notify()\neffect ping\nmutation ping():\n  emit ping {{}}\n")).unwrap();
    let auth = effect_token(&f, false);
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules", "--clock-offset", "+25h"]);
    assert_eq!(host.post("/a/notify?wait=0", json!({"id":"action-live", "args":{}}))["state"], "pending");
    let claimed = host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/claim", json!({"lease":120, "session":60}), &auth);
    let action = &claimed["requests"][0];
    assert_eq!(action["attempt"], 1);
    let token = action["session"]["token"].as_str().unwrap();
    let viewer = format!("Authorization: Bearer {token}");
    let settle_auth = format!("{auth}\r\nx-snapback-action-lease: {token}");
    let original_expiry = action["session"]["expiresAt"].as_i64().unwrap();
    let conn = rusqlite::Connection::open(f.0.join(".snapback4/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(host.post("/m/ping", json!({"args":{}}))["state"], "sent");
    let effect = host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({"lease":60}), &auth)["effects"][0].clone();
    let effect_deadline = || conn.query_row("SELECT lease_until FROM _outbox", [], |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0)).unwrap();
    let original_effect_expiry = effect_deadline();
    let deadlines = || conn.query_row("SELECT r.lease_until, s.expires_at FROM _requests r JOIN _sessions s ON s.token_digest = r.session_digest WHERE r.id = 'action-live'", [], |row| Ok((row.get::<_, i64>(0)?, row.get::<_, i64>(1)?))).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(deadlines(), (original_expiry, original_expiry));
    let service_expiry = || conn.query_row("SELECT expires_at FROM _sessions WHERE kind = 'service'", [], |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0)).unwrap();
    let original_service_expiry = service_expiry();
    assert_eq!(host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"9007199254740991ms"}))["denied"]["code"], "E_CLOCK_INPUT");
    assert_eq!(deadlines(), (original_expiry, original_expiry), "invalid advances leave every deadline untouched");
    // Force the last lease update to fail: sessions, effects and the clock
    // must all retain their earlier deadlines before an ordinary retry.
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TRIGGER refuse_action_shift BEFORE UPDATE OF lease_until ON _requests BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'test refuses action shift'); END;").unwrap();
    let wall = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let before = host.query("time").as_i64().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"120s"}))["denied"]["code"], "E_STORE");
    assert_eq!(deadlines(), (original_expiry, original_expiry));
    assert_eq!(effect_deadline(), original_effect_expiry);
    let elapsed = host.query("time").as_i64().unwrap() - before;
    // `wall` was read before `before` and this read follows the second server read, so the server
    // interval is nested inside the wall interval: load cannot fail this, an applied 120 s advance cannot pass it.
    let bracket = snapback4_core::now_ms() - wall;
    assert!(elapsed <= bracket + 50 && bracket < 100_000, "a refused lease transaction must not advance the clock: server {elapsed} ms inside a wall bracket of {bracket} ms");
    conn.execute_batch("DROP TRIGGER refuse_action_shift;").unwrap();
    let start = Instant::now();
    let port = host.address.rsplit(':').next().unwrap();
    let advanced = output_json(f.command(&["clock", "advance", "120s", "--port", port]));
    assert_eq!(advanced["advancedMs"], 120_000);
    let state: String = conn.query_row("SELECT state FROM _requests WHERE id = 'action-live'", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
    println!("action clock: after_advance_state={state}");
    assert_eq!(state, "running", "clock advance must not sweep the worker's live action claim");
    assert_eq!(deadlines(), (original_expiry + 120_000, original_expiry + 120_000));
    assert_eq!(host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"120s"}))["advancedMs"], 120_000);
    assert_eq!(deadlines(), (original_expiry + 240_000, original_expiry + 240_000));
    assert_eq!(effect_deadline(), original_effect_expiry + 240_000);
    assert_eq!(service_expiry(), original_service_expiry, "an advance does not renew ordinary service credentials");
    assert!(host.auth_request("POST", "/q/time", json!({"args":{}}), &viewer)["data"].is_number(), "the original viewer session remains usable");
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/claim", json!({}), &auth)["requests"], json!([]), "another claim cannot steal the running request");
    let settled = host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/action-live/done", json!({"result":"sent once"}), &settle_auth);
    assert_eq!(settled["ok"], true, "{settled}");
    assert_eq!(settled["request"]["state"], "done");
    assert_eq!(settled["request"]["result"], "sent once");
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/q/time", json!({"args":{}}), &viewer)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "settlement still revokes the original viewer session");
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", &format!("/effects/{}/done", effect["id"].as_str().unwrap()), json!({}), &auth)["ok"], true);
    println!("action clock: advances=2 advanced_ms=240000 lease_ms=60000 session_extension_ms=240000 requeues=0 attempts=1 wall_ms={}", start.elapsed().as_millis());
}

#[test]
fn action_retry_times_follow_dev_clock_and_real_expiry_still_fences_dev_and_serve() {
    for release in [false, true] {
        let f = Fixture::new(if release { "action-expiry-release" } else { "action-expiry-dev" });
        fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("use server\n{SOURCE}\naction notify()\n")).unwrap();
        if release { assert!(f.command(&["deploy", "deployment"]).status.success()); }
        let auth = effect_token(&f, release);
        let host = f.start(if release { &["serve", "deployment"] } else { &["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"] });
        assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/a/notify?wait=0", json!({"id":"expiry", "args":{}}), &auth)["state"], "pending");
        let first = host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/claim", json!({"lease":1, "session":1}), &auth)["requests"][0].clone();
        let token = first["session"]["token"].as_str().unwrap();
        let expiry = first["session"]["expiresAt"].as_i64().unwrap();
        let start = Instant::now();
        while snapback4_core::now_ms() <= expiry { std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)); }
        if !release { assert_eq!(host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"120s"}))["advancedMs"], 120_000); }
        assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/q/time", json!({"args":{}}), &format!("Authorization: Bearer {token}"))["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "an overdue action session stays overdue after an advance");
        let second = host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/claim", json!({}), &auth)["requests"][0].clone();
        assert_eq!(second["attempt"], 2);
        let refused = host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/expiry/done", json!({}), &format!("{auth}\r\nx-snapback-action-lease: {token}"));
        assert_eq!(refused["denied"]["code"], "E_ACTION_LEASE");
        let second_auth = format!("{auth}\r\nx-snapback-action-lease: {}", second["session"]["token"].as_str().unwrap());
        println!("action expiry: release={release} lease_ms=1000 requeues=1 attempts=2 wall_ms={}", start.elapsed().as_millis());
        if release { assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/expiry/done", json!({}), &second_auth)["ok"], true); }
        else {
            assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/expiry/failed", json!({"error":"provider unavailable", "retry":true}), &second_auth)["ok"], true);
            let conn = rusqlite::Connection::open(f.0.join(".snapback4/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
            let next_at = || conn.query_row("SELECT next_at FROM _requests WHERE id = 'expiry'", [], |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0)).unwrap();
            let next = next_at();
            assert_eq!(host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"4s"}))["advancedMs"], 4_000);
            assert_eq!(next_at(), next, "an advance does not postpone a queued retry");
            let third = host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/claim", json!({}), &auth)["requests"][0].clone();
            assert_eq!(third["attempt"], 3);
            assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/actions/expiry/done", json!({}), &format!("{auth}\r\nx-snapback-action-lease: {}", third["session"]["token"].as_str().unwrap()))["ok"], true);
            println!("action retry: advanced_ms=4000 attempts=3 next_at_shift_ms=0");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn effect_retry_next_at_still_follows_the_dev_clock() {
    let f = effect_fixture("effect-retry");
    let auth = effect_token(&f, false);
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules", "--clock-offset", "+25h"]);
    host.post("/m/notify", json!({"id":"notify", "args":{}}));
    let claimed = host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth);
    let id = claimed["effects"][0]["id"].as_str().unwrap();
    let before = host.query("time").as_i64().unwrap();
    let failed = host.auth_request("POST", &format!("/effects/{id}/failed"), json!({"error":"try again", "retry":true}), &auth);
    let next = failed["effect"]["nextAt"].as_i64().unwrap();
    assert!(next >= before + 4_000 && next <= host.query("time").as_i64().unwrap() + 4_000, "{failed}");
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth)["effects"], json!([]));
    let start = Instant::now();
    assert_eq!(host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"4s"}))["advancedMs"], 4_000);
    let retry = host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth);
    assert_eq!(retry["effects"][0]["id"], id, "{retry}");
    assert_eq!(retry["effects"][0]["attempt"], 2);
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", &format!("/effects/{id}/done"), json!({}), &auth)["ok"], true);
    println!("effect retry: backoff_ms=4000 advanced_ms=4000 attempts=2 wall_ms={}", start.elapsed().as_millis());
}

#[test]
fn real_time_effect_lease_loss_requeues_in_dev_and_serve() {
    for release in [false, true] {
        let f = effect_fixture(if release { "effect-wall-release" } else { "effect-wall-dev" });
        if release { assert!(f.command(&["deploy", "deployment"]).status.success()); }
        let auth = effect_token(&f, release);
        let host = f.start(if release { &["serve", "deployment"] } else { &["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"] });
        assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/m/notify", json!({"id":"notify", "args":{}}), &auth)["state"], "sent");
        let claimed = host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({"lease":1}), &auth);
        let id = claimed["effects"][0]["id"].as_str().unwrap();
        let start = Instant::now();
        loop {
            let effects = host.auth_request("GET", "/effects", json!(null), &auth);
            if effects["effects"][0]["state"] == "queued" { break; }
            assert!(start.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(20), "{effects}");
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50));
        }
        let late = host.auth_request("POST", &format!("/effects/{id}/done"), json!({}), &auth);
        assert_eq!(late["denied"]["code"], "E_EFFECT_LEASE", "{late}");
        let retry = host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth);
        assert_eq!(retry["effects"][0]["id"], id);
        assert_eq!(retry["effects"][0]["attempt"], 2);
        assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", &format!("/effects/{id}/done"), json!({}), &auth)["ok"], true);
        println!("effect wall: release={release} lease_ms=1000 requeues=1 attempts=2 wall_ms={}", start.elapsed().as_millis());
    }
}


#[test]
fn grace_push_is_deferred_until_clock_advance_then_delivered_once() {
    let f = effect_fixture("grace-push");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("{SOURCE}\neffect notify\nmutation push():\n  insert counters {{n: 1, at: now}}\n  if not exists connected[viewer]:\n    emit notify {{recipient: viewer}}\n")).unwrap();
    let auth = effect_token(&f, false);
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
    host.request("GET", "/changes?wait=0", json!(null));
    assert_eq!(host.post("/m/push", json!({"id":"push", "args":{}}))["state"], "sent");
    let admitted_by = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let queued = host.auth_request("GET", "/effects", json!(null), &auth);
    assert_eq!(queued["effects"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1, "grace must defer the push, not lose it: {queued}");
    let effect = &queued["effects"][0];
    let delay = effect["nextAt"].as_i64().unwrap() - effect["at"].as_i64().unwrap();
    // Presence samples its clock after the mutation's `at` was captured.
    // Admission completion brackets that sample without adding clock tolerance.
    assert!(delay > 0 && effect["nextAt"].as_i64().unwrap() <= admitted_by + 35_000, "{effect}");
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth)["effects"], json!([]));
    let before = output_json(f.command(&["doctor", "--json"]));
    let lines = before["lines"].as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|v| v.as_str().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
    assert!(lines.contains("1 deferred (next at "), "{lines}");
    assert!(lines.contains(&effect["nextAt"].to_string()), "{lines}");
    let start = Instant::now();
    let port = host.address.rsplit(':').next().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(output_json(f.command(&["clock", "advance", "36s", "--port", port]))["advancedMs"], 36_000);
    let after = output_json(f.command(&["doctor", "--json"]));
    let lines = after["lines"].as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|v| v.as_str().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
    assert!(lines.contains("0 deferred"), "{lines}");
    let claimed = host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth);
    assert_eq!(claimed["effects"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1, "{claimed}");
    assert_eq!(claimed["effects"][0]["id"], effect["id"]);
    assert_eq!(claimed["effects"][0]["attempt"], 1);
    let id = effect["id"].as_str().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", &format!("/effects/{id}/done"), json!({}), &auth)["ok"], true);
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth)["effects"], json!([]));
    assert_eq!(host.post("/m/push", json!({"id":"push", "args":{}}))["replayed"], true);
    assert_eq!(host.query("counters").as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    println!("grace push: delay_ms={delay} advanced_ms=36000 delivered=1 attempts=1 duplicate_claims=0 wall_ms={}", start.elapsed().as_millis());
}


#[test]
fn grace_push_reconnect_is_satisfied_even_after_restart_and_disconnect() {
    for reconnect in [false, true] {
        let f = effect_fixture(if reconnect { "grace-reconnect" } else { "grace-restart" });
        fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("{SOURCE}\neffect notify\nmutation push():\n  if not exists connected[viewer]:\n    emit notify {{recipient: viewer}}\n")).unwrap();
        let auth = effect_token(&f, false);
        let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
        host.request("GET", "/changes?wait=0", json!(null));
        assert_eq!(host.post("/m/push", json!({"id":"push", "args":{}}))["state"], "sent");
        let queued = host.auth_request("GET", "/effects", json!(null), &auth)["effects"][0].clone();
        assert_eq!(queued["state"], "queued");
        if reconnect { host.request("GET", "/changes?wait=0", json!(null)); }
        drop(host);
        let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
        assert_eq!(host.auth_request("GET", "/effects", json!(null), &auth)["effects"][0]["nextAt"], queued["nextAt"]);
        assert_eq!(host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"36s"}))["advancedMs"], 36_000);
        let claimed = host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth);
        if reconnect {
            assert_eq!(claimed["effects"], json!([]));
            let effect = host.auth_request("GET", "/effects?state=satisfied", json!(null), &auth)["effects"][0].clone();
            assert_eq!(effect["id"], queued["id"]);
            assert_eq!(effect["attempt"], 0);
            assert_eq!(effect["result"], json!({"outcome":"presence_reconnected"}));
        } else {
            assert_eq!(claimed["effects"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
            assert_eq!(claimed["effects"][0]["id"], queued["id"]);
            let id = queued["id"].as_str().unwrap();
            assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", &format!("/effects/{id}/done"), json!({}), &auth)["ok"], true);
        }
        assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth)["effects"], json!([]));
        println!("grace HTTP restart: reconnect={reconnect} delivered={} attempts={}", u8::from(!reconnect), u8::from(!reconnect));
    }
}

#[test]
fn grace_push_serve_keeps_deadline_and_reconnect_record_for_claim_after_restart() {
    let f = effect_fixture("grace-serve");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "effect notify\nmutation push():\n  if not exists connected[viewer]:\n    emit notify {recipient: viewer}\n").unwrap();
    assert!(f.command(&["deploy", "deployment"]).status.success());
    let auth = effect_token(&f, true);
    let host = f.start(&["serve", "deployment"]);
    // No connection/grace: immediate, also on the release host.
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/m/push", json!({"id":"immediate", "args":{}}), &auth)["state"], "sent");
    let immediate = host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth)["effects"][0].clone();
    assert_eq!(immediate["state"], "running");
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", &format!("/effects/{}/done", immediate["id"].as_str().unwrap()), json!({}), &auth)["ok"], true);
    host.auth_request("GET", "/changes?wait=0", json!(null), &auth);
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/m/push", json!({"id":"deferred", "args":{}}), &auth)["state"], "sent");
    let admitted_by = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let queued = host.auth_request("GET", "/effects?state=queued", json!(null), &auth)["effects"][0].clone();
    let deadline = queued["nextAt"].as_i64().unwrap();
    assert!(deadline > queued["at"].as_i64().unwrap() && deadline <= admitted_by + 35_000, "{queued}");
    assert_eq!(host.auth_request("POST", "/effects/claim", json!({}), &auth)["effects"], json!([]));
    host.auth_request("GET", "/changes?wait=0", json!(null), &auth);
    drop(host);
    // Reopen the real release store and pin claim time at its durable
    // deadline; production has no clock-control route and needs no sleep.
    let mut engine = snapback4_core::Engine::open(snapback4_core::Store::open(&f.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert!(engine.claim_effects("service:worker", None, 10, 60_000, deadline).unwrap().is_empty());
    let satisfied = engine.effects(Some("satisfied"), 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(satisfied.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(satisfied[0].attempts, 0);
    assert_eq!(satisfied[0].result.as_ref().unwrap().field("outcome").unwrap().as_str().unwrap(), "presence_reconnected");
    println!("grace serve: immediate_delivered=1 deferred_delivered=0 satisfied=1 attempts=0 restarts=1");
}

#[test]
fn maintained_column_activation_uses_offset_clock_in_watch_startup_and_cli() {
    const BEFORE: &str = "use personas alice\ntable posts:\n  at: time\n  expiresAt: time ?\n  by byExpiry: expiresAt, id\n  expire at .expiresAt\n  public\n  sync public\ntable likes:\n  postId: posts\n  public\n  online only\nquery one():\n  return posts['p']\nquery time():\n  return now\n";
    let after = BEFORE.replacen("  at: time", "  at: time\n  likeCount: count likes by postId", 1);
    for path in ["watch", "startup", "cli"] {
        let f = Fixture::new(&format!("column-{path}"));
        fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), BEFORE).unwrap();
        let host = f.start(&["dev", "--clock-offset", "-2h", "--hold-schedules"]);
        let now = host.query("time").as_i64().unwrap();
        let expires = now + 3_600_000;
        assert!(now < expires && expires < snapback4_core::now_ms());
        let seeded = host.post("/seed", json!([
            {"table":"posts", "row":{"id":"p", "at":17, "expiresAt":expires}},
            {"table":"likes", "row":{"id":"l", "postId":"p"}}
        ]));
        assert_eq!(seeded["seeded"], 2, "{seeded}");
        if path == "watch" {
            fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), &after).unwrap();
            let end = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(15);
            loop {
                let row = host.query("one");
                if row["likeCount"] == 1 { assert_eq!(row["at"], 17); break; }
                assert!(Instant::now() < end, "offset-clock adoption did not mirror live parent: {row}");
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
            }
        } else {
            drop(host);
            fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), &after).unwrap();
            if path == "cli" {
                let reply = output_json(f.command(&["run", "one", "--as", "alice", "--now", &now.to_string()]));
                assert_eq!(reply["data"]["likeCount"], 1, "{reply}");
            } else {
                let host = f.start(&["dev", "--clock-offset", "-2h", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
                assert_eq!(host.query("one")["likeCount"], 1);
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn future_store_explains_itself_at_restart_and_doctor_but_not_under_the_same_offset() {
    let f = Fixture::new("store-ahead");
    // Ordinary expiry and a future appointment are not evidence of a clock mistake.
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("{SOURCE}\nmutation appointment():\n  return insert counters {{n: 0, at: now + 864000000}}\nmutation fixed():\n  return insert counters {{id: 'fixed', n: 0, at: now}}\n")).unwrap();
    let start = |args: &[&str]| {
        let log = f.0.join("startup.log");
        let mut child = Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&f.0).args(args).args(["--port", "0"])
            .stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(fs::File::create(&log).unwrap()).spawn().unwrap();
        let mut line = String::new(); BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().unwrap()).read_line(&mut line).unwrap();
        let text = fs::read_to_string(log).unwrap();
        assert!(!line.is_empty(), "{text}");
        (Host { child, address: line.trim().trim_start_matches("http://").into() }, text)
    };
    let (host, log) = start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
    assert!(!log.contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "{log}");
    host.post("/m/post", json!({"id":"ordinary","args":{}}));
    host.post("/m/appointment", json!({"id":"appointment","args":{}}));
    let doctor = output_json(f.command(&["doctor", "--json"]));
    assert!(doctor["lines"].as_array().unwrap().iter().any(|v| v.as_str().unwrap().contains("offset from wall time:")));
    assert!(!doctor.to_string().contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "{doctor}");
    drop(host);
    let (host, log) = start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
    assert!(!log.contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "{log}");
    let jump = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"25h"}));
    assert!((jump["offsetMs"].as_i64().unwrap() - 90_000_000).abs() < 100, "{jump}");
    assert!(jump["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("lasts until restart"));
    let guidance = jump["message"].as_str().unwrap();
    assert!(guidance.contains("dev --memory --port") && !guidance.contains("dev --fresh --port 4401"), "{guidance}");
    if let (Ok(lo), Ok(hi)) = (std::env::var("PORT_LO"), std::env::var("PORT_HI")) {
        assert!(guidance.contains(&format!("--port {lo}-{hi}")), "{guidance}");
    } else { assert!(guidance.contains("--port 0"), "{guidance}"); }
    host.post("/m/post", json!({"id":"future","args":{}}));
    host.post("/m/fixed", json!({"id":"fixed","args":{}}));
    drop(host);
    let (host, log) = start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules", "--clock-offset", "+25h"]);
    assert!(!log.contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "{log}"); drop(host);
    let (host, log) = start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
    assert_eq!(log.matches("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD").count(), 1, "{log}");
    assert!(log.contains("counters, stories"), "{log}");
    assert!(log.contains("up to ") && log.contains("ms (latest 64 commits; tables:") && log.contains("same --clock-offset"), "{log}");
    let doctor = output_json(f.command(&["doctor", "--json"]));
    assert!(doctor.to_string().contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "{doctor}");
    drop(host);
    let doctor = output_json(f.command(&["doctor", "--json"]));
    assert!(doctor.to_string().contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "{doctor}");
    assert!(doctor.to_string().contains("no running dev"));
    let (host, log) = start(&["dev", "--fresh", "--no-watch"]);
    assert!(!log.contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "{log}");
    assert_eq!(host.query("counters"), json!([]));
    let doctor = output_json(f.command(&["doctor", "--json", "--url", &format!("http://{}", host.address)]));
    assert!(!doctor.to_string().contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "{doctor}");
    drop(host);
    let (_host, log) = start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
    assert!(log.contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "fresh testing must preserve the main store: {log}");
}

#[test]
fn matching_appointment_values_never_become_commit_clock_evidence() {
    let f = Fixture::new("appointment-control");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\ntable history:\n  at: time\n  public\n  insert <- allow\n  delete <- allow\n  online only\ntable appointments:\n  at: time\n  public\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add():\n  return insert history {at: 0}\nmutation drop(id: history):\n  delete history[id]\nmutation appointment(at: time):\n  return insert appointments {at}\n").unwrap();
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules", "--clock-offset", "25h"]);
    let added = host.post("/m/add", json!({"id":"future","args":{}}));
    assert_eq!(added["state"], "sent", "{added}");
    let stamp: i64 = rusqlite::Connection::open_with_flags(f.0.join(".snapback4/store.sqlite"), rusqlite::OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY).unwrap()
        .query_row("SELECT at FROM _commits WHERE program = 'add'", [], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(host.post("/m/drop", json!({"id":"drop","args":{"id":added["result"]["id"]}}))["state"], "sent");
    drop(host);
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
    // Historical commit evidence persists independently of retained rows. The
    // matching ordinary appointment must never join the affected-table list.
    let before = host.request("GET", "/dev/clock/status", json!(null));
    assert!(before["storeAhead"].as_str().unwrap().contains("tables: history)"), "{before}");
    assert_eq!(host.post("/m/appointment", json!({"id":"appointment","args":{"at":stamp}}))["state"], "sent");
    let after = host.request("GET", "/dev/clock/status", json!(null));
    assert!(after["storeAhead"].as_str().unwrap().contains("tables: history)"), "{after}");
    assert!(!after["storeAhead"].as_str().unwrap().contains("appointments"));
    // The explicit bounded-window limit: after 64 normal commits, no evidence
    // remains in the window, although future appointment values still exist.
    for n in 1..64 {
        assert_eq!(host.post("/m/appointment", json!({"id":format!("appointment-{n}"),"args":{"at":stamp}}))["state"], "sent");
    }
    assert!(host.request("GET", "/dev/clock/status", json!(null))["storeAhead"].is_null());
    drop(host);
    let _host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
    assert!(!output_json(f.command(&["doctor", "--json"])).to_string().contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"));
}

#[test]
fn startup_clock_advice_ignores_bad_row_images_and_reports_inspection_failure() {
    for bad_commit in [false, true] {
        let f = Fixture::new(if bad_commit { "bad-commit" } else { "bad-image" });
        fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\ntable notes:\n  at: time\n  public\n  online only\n").unwrap();
        let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules", "--clock-offset", "25h"]);
        assert_eq!(host.post("/seed", json!([{"table":"notes","row":{"id":"n","at":0}}]))["seeded"], 1);
        drop(host);
        let conn = rusqlite::Connection::open(f.0.join(".snapback4/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
        conn.execute("UPDATE r SET data = 'unparsable row image'", []).unwrap();
        if bad_commit { conn.execute("UPDATE _commits SET at = 'unparsable commit stamp'", []).unwrap(); }
        drop(conn);
        let log = f.0.join("startup.log");
        let mut child = Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&f.0).args(["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules", "--port", "0"])
            .stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(fs::File::create(&log).unwrap()).spawn().unwrap();
        let mut line = String::new(); BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().unwrap()).read_line(&mut line).unwrap();
        let text = fs::read_to_string(&log).unwrap();
        assert!(line.starts_with("http://"), "{text}");
        let _host = Host { child, address: line.trim().trim_start_matches("http://").into() };
        if bad_commit {
            assert!(text.contains("store-ahead inspection unavailable"), "{text}");
            assert!(!text.contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD"), "{text}");
        } else {
            assert!(text.contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD") && text.contains("tables: notes"), "{text}");
            assert!(!text.contains("store-ahead inspection unavailable"), "{text}");
        }
    }
}

// Farm round 2 (dev-clock cluster): an advance names why each schedule did or
// did not fire, and `--resume-clock` continues a store's time after a restart.
#[test]
fn advance_explains_each_schedule_and_resume_clock_releases_a_restart_hold() {
    let f = Fixture::new("explain");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\ntable counters:\n  n: int\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add():\n  insert counters { n: 0 }\nquery counters():\n  return counters first 10 by byId\njob tick(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):\n  for c in counters:\n    update counters[c.id] { n: c.n + 1 }\nschedule ticker every 1h: tick\njob morningTick(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):\n  for c in counters:\n    update counters[c.id] { n: c.n }\nschedule morning every 1d at 06:00: morningTick\n").unwrap();
    // Daily-boundary explanation is independent of the hourly counter: starting
    // before 06:00 UTC used to run tick an extra time and make the final n=2 fail.
    let start = |args: &[&str]| {
        let log = f.0.join("startup.log");
        let mut child = Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&f.0).args(args).args(["--port", "0"])
            .stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(fs::File::create(&log).unwrap()).spawn().unwrap();
        let mut line = String::new(); BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().unwrap()).read_line(&mut line).unwrap();
        let text = fs::read_to_string(log).unwrap();
        assert!(!line.is_empty(), "{text}");
        (Host { child, address: line.trim().trim_start_matches("http://").into() }, text)
    };
    let (host, _) = start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
    assert_eq!(host.post("/m/add", json!({"id":"a","args":{}}))["state"], "sent");
    let advanced = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"5h"}));
    let ticker = &advanced["schedules"][0];
    assert_eq!((ticker["name"].as_str(), ticker["fired"].as_bool(), ticker["skippedPeriods"].as_i64()), (Some("ticker"), Some(true), Some(4)), "{advanced}");
    assert_eq!(ticker["periodEnd"].as_i64().unwrap() - ticker["periodStart"].as_i64().unwrap(), 3_600_000);
    assert_eq!(ticker["run"]["state"], "done", "{advanced}");
    assert!(ticker["reason"].as_str().unwrap().contains("missed boundaries fire once"), "{advanced}");
    // Count firings of the counter's target. The daily explanation fixture
    // has its own target and may fire during either advance without changing n.
    let advanced_ticks = advanced["schedules"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|schedule| schedule["fired"] == true && schedule["target"] == "tick").count();
    let morning = &advanced["schedules"][1];
    assert_eq!(morning["period"], "every 1d at 06:00 UTC");
    if morning["fired"] == false { assert!(morning["reason"].as_str().unwrap().contains("schedule times are UTC"), "{advanced}"); }
    assert_eq!(host.query("counters")[0]["n"], 1, "{advanced}");
    drop(host);
    let (host, log) = start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
    assert!(log.contains("schedule ticker is held until") && log.contains("--resume-clock"), "{log}");
    let held = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"2h"}));
    assert_eq!(held["work"]["schedules"], 0, "{held}");
    let ticker = &held["schedules"][0];
    assert_eq!(ticker["fired"], false);
    assert!(ticker["reason"].as_str().unwrap().contains("held by the store") && ticker["heldUntil"].is_string(), "{held}");
    let doctor = output_json(f.command(&["doctor", "--json"]));
    assert!(doctor.to_string().contains("ahead of the clock: held until"), "{doctor}");
    drop(host);
    let refused = f.command(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--resume-clock", "--fresh", "--port", "0"]);
    assert!(!refused.status.success() && String::from_utf8_lossy(&refused.stderr).contains("cannot be combined"), "{refused:?}");
    let (host, log) = start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--resume-clock"]);
    assert!(log.contains("--resume-clock: offset +") && !log.contains("N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD") && !log.contains("is held until"), "{log}");
    let resumed = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"1h"}));
    assert_eq!(resumed["schedules"][0]["fired"], true, "{resumed}");
    let resumed_ticks = resumed["schedules"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|schedule| schedule["fired"] == true && schedule["target"] == "tick").count();
    assert_eq!(host.query("counters")[0]["n"], advanced_ticks + resumed_ticks, "{resumed}");
}

// Wave 4 of the app farm: the time card's look-back sweep catches up
// rows that fell due in boundaries an advance coalesced into one run.
#[test]
fn the_time_cards_look_back_sweep_catches_up_coalesced_boundaries() {
    let card = snapback4_lang::guide::card("time").unwrap();
    let block = snapback4_lang::guide::quarry(card).into_iter().find(|b| b.contains("job closeDeparted(")).unwrap();
    let f = Fixture::new("catch-up");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("use personas alice\n{block}mutation add(name: text <=40, departsAt: time):\n  insert trains {{ name, departsAt }}\nquery clock():\n  return now\nquery open():\n  return trains where .open first 50 by .id\n")).unwrap();
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
    let now = host.query("clock").as_i64().unwrap();
    for (n, minutes) in [10, 50, 170].into_iter().enumerate() {
        let write = host.post("/m/add", json!({"id": format!("w{n}"), "args": {"name": format!("t{n}"), "departsAt": now + minutes * 60_000}}));
        assert_eq!(write["state"], "sent", "{write}");
    }
    let advanced = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"3h"}));
    let closer = &advanced["schedules"][0];
    assert_eq!(closer["fired"], true, "{advanced}");
    assert!(closer["skippedPeriods"].as_i64().unwrap() > 100, "one run stands for every coalesced minute: {advanced}");
    assert_eq!(host.query("open"), json!([]), "rows due in skipped periods are closed too");
}

#[test]
fn clock_discovers_custom_port_memory_and_survives_reset_identity_rotation() {
    let f=Fixture::new("discover-memory");
    let host=f.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    let before=host.query("time").as_i64().unwrap();
    let advanced=output_json(f.command(&["clock","advance","+2h"]));
    assert!(advanced["now"].as_i64().unwrap()>=before+7_200_000,"{advanced}");
    let identity=host.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null);
    host.post("/dev/reset",json!({}));
    let after=host.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null);
    assert_ne!(identity["storeId"],after["storeId"]);
    assert_eq!(identity["initialStoreId"],after["initialStoreId"]);
    output_json(f.command(&["clock","advance","1s"]));
}

#[test]
fn clock_ignores_crashed_records_and_startup_reclaims_them() {
    let f=Fixture::new("discover-stale");
    let stale=f.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    let dir=f.0.join(".snapback4/dev-hosts");
    let path=fs::read_dir(&dir).unwrap().next().unwrap().unwrap().path();
    drop(stale); // SIGKILL leaves its immutable registration.
    assert!(path.exists());
    let missing=f.command(&["clock","advance","1s"]);
    assert!(!missing.status.success());
    let _live=f.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    assert!(!path.exists());
    output_json(f.command(&["clock","advance","1s"]));
}

#[test]
fn clock_refuses_ambiguous_memory_and_persisted_hosts_without_mutating_either() {
    for persisted in [false,true] {
        let f=Fixture::new(&format!("discover-multiple-{persisted}"));
        let a=if persisted {f.start(&["dev","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"])} else {f.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"])};
        let b=f.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
        let before_a=a.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null)["clockOffsetMs"].as_i64().unwrap();
        let before_b=b.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null)["clockOffsetMs"].as_i64().unwrap();
        let out=f.command(&["clock","advance","2h"]);
        assert!(!out.status.success());
        let error=String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
        assert!(error.contains("multiple live dev hosts") && error.contains(&a.address) && error.contains(&b.address),"{error}");
        assert!((a.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null)["clockOffsetMs"].as_i64().unwrap()-before_a).abs()<100);
        assert!((b.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null)["clockOffsetMs"].as_i64().unwrap()-before_b).abs()<100);
        output_json(f.command(&["clock","advance","1s","--port",b.address.rsplit(':').next().unwrap()]));
    }
}

#[test]
fn clock_ignores_live_registration_with_wrong_project_or_reused_instance() {
    use std::fs::OpenOptions;
    let f=Fixture::new("discover-forged");
    let other=Fixture::new("discover-foreign");
    let foreign=other.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    let local=f.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    let dir=f.0.join(".snapback4/dev-hosts");
    for entry in fs::read_dir(&dir).unwrap() {fs::remove_file(entry.unwrap().path()).unwrap();}
    for project_matches in [false,true] {
        let instance=if project_matches {"wronginstance"} else {"wrongproject"};
        let path=dir.join(format!("{instance}.json"));
        let target=if project_matches {&local} else {&foreign};
        let mut record=target.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null);
        let record=json!({"instance":instance,"initialStoreId":record["initialStoreId"].take(),"project":if project_matches {json!(f.0.canonicalize().unwrap())}else{record["project"].take()},"address":target.address,"temporary":true,"pid":record["pid"].take()});
        fs::write(&path,record.to_string()).unwrap();
        let held=OpenOptions::new().read(true).write(true).open(&path).unwrap();held.lock().unwrap();
        let out=f.command(&["clock","advance","2h"]);
        assert!(!out.status.success());
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("no running dev discovered"));
        assert!(target.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null)["clockOffsetMs"].as_i64().unwrap().abs()<100);
    }
}

#[test]
fn stale_instance_control_is_refused_before_clock_or_reset_changes() {
    let f=Fixture::new("discover-instance-race");
    let host=f.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    let before=host.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null);
    for (path,body) in [("/dev/clock/advance",json!({"duration":"2h"})),("/dev/reset",json!({}))] {
        let reply=host.auth_request("POST",path,body,"x-snapback-dev-instance: stale-instance");
        assert_eq!(reply["denied"]["code"],"E_INPUT","{reply}");
        let after=host.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null);
        assert_eq!(after["storeId"],before["storeId"]);
        assert!(after["clockOffsetMs"].as_i64().unwrap().abs()<100);
    }
}

#[test]
fn wildcard_memory_hosts_are_discovered_and_participate_in_ambiguity_and_cleanup() {
    for bind in ["0.0.0.0","::"] {
        let f=Fixture::new(&format!("discover-wildcard-{bind}"));
        let wildcard=f.start(&["dev","--memory","--bind",bind,"--i-understand-cleartext","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
        let dir=f.0.join(".snapback4/dev-hosts");
        let path=fs::read_dir(&dir).unwrap().next().unwrap().unwrap().path();
        let record:Value=serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(&path).unwrap()).unwrap();
        let address:std::net::SocketAddr=record["address"].as_str().unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        assert!(address.ip().is_loopback());assert_eq!(address.is_ipv4(),bind=="0.0.0.0");
        output_json(f.command(&["clock","advance","1s"]));
        let persisted=f.start(&["dev","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
        let out=f.command(&["reset"]);assert!(!out.status.success());
        let error=String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
        assert!(error.contains("multiple live dev hosts") && error.contains(&address.to_string()) && error.contains(&persisted.address),"{error}");
        drop(wildcard);assert!(path.exists());drop(persisted);
        let _next=f.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
        assert!(!path.exists());
        output_json(f.command(&["clock","advance","1s"]));
    }
}

#[test]
fn stale_legacy_foreign_port_cannot_veto_a_valid_memory_registration() {
    let f=Fixture::new("discover-stale-legacy");
    drop(f.start(&["dev","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]));
    let other=Fixture::new("discover-stale-legacy-other");
    let foreign=other.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    fs::write(f.0.join(".snapback4/serve.lock"),format!("{}\nhttp://{}\n",foreign.address.rsplit(':').next().unwrap(),foreign.address)).unwrap();
    let local=f.start(&["dev","--memory","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    output_json(f.command(&["clock","advance","2h"]));
    assert!(f.command(&["doctor","--latency"]).status.success());
    output_json(f.command(&["reset","--clock","reset"]));
    assert!(local.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null)["clockOffsetMs"].as_i64().unwrap().abs()<100);
    assert!(foreign.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null)["clockOffsetMs"].as_i64().unwrap().abs()<100);
    let url=format!("http://{}",foreign.address);
    let explicit=f.command(&["doctor","--url",&url]);assert!(!explicit.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&explicit.stderr).contains("serves project"));
}

#[test]
fn persisted_restart_nonce_refuses_old_latency_reset_even_with_the_same_store_id() {
    let f=Fixture::new("discover-latency-instance-race");
    let first=f.start(&["dev","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    let old=first.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null);
    let port=first.address.rsplit(':').next().unwrap().to_owned();drop(first);
    let second=f.start_on_port(&["dev","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"],&port);
    let current=second.request("GET","/dev/status",Value::Null);
    assert_eq!(old["storeId"],current["storeId"]);assert_ne!(old["instance"],current["instance"]);
    second.query("time");
    let path=format!("/dev/latency/reset?store_id={}",old["storeId"].as_str().unwrap());
    let deadline=Instant::now()+Duration::from_secs(3);
    let before=loop {
        let snapshot=second.request("GET","/dev/latency",Value::Null);
        if snapshot["operations"]["/q/time"]["n"]==1 {break snapshot;}
        assert!(Instant::now()<deadline,"query latency did not settle: {snapshot}");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1));
    };
    let reply=second.auth_request("POST",&path,Value::Null,&format!("x-snapback-dev-instance: {}",old["instance"].as_str().unwrap()));
    assert_eq!(reply["denied"]["code"],"E_INPUT","{reply}");
    assert_eq!(second.request("GET","/dev/latency",Value::Null),before);
    let cleared=output_json(f.command(&["doctor","--latency","--reset","--json"]));
    assert_eq!(cleared["operations"]["/q/time"]["n"],0);
}

#[test]
fn kit3_clock_reports_skipped_windows_and_each_boundary_preserves_order() {
    let f = Fixture::new("kit3-windows");
    let source = "use personas alice\ntable windows:\n  start: time\n  end: time\n  at: time\n  public 'test'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ntable drivers:\n  n: int\n  cap 1\n  public 'test'\n  online only\nquery windows():\n  return windows first 100 by .id\njob record(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):\n  for d in drivers:\n    insert windows {start: periodStart, end: periodEnd, at: now}\nschedule minute every 1m: record\nschedule two every 2m: record\n";
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), source).unwrap();
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
    let port = host.address.rsplit(':').next().unwrap();
    host.post("/seed", json!([{"table":"drivers","row":{"id":"one","n":0}}]));
    let advance = output_json(f.command(&["clock", "advance", "3m", "--port", port]));
    assert!(advance["skewMs"].as_i64().unwrap() >= 180_000 - 100, "{advance}");
    let minute = advance["schedules"].as_array().unwrap().iter().find(|s| s["name"] == "minute").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(minute["firedWindows"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(minute["skippedBoundaries"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 2);
    for skipped in minute["skippedBoundaries"].as_array().unwrap() {
        assert_eq!(skipped["periodEnd"].as_i64().unwrap() - skipped["periodStart"].as_i64().unwrap(), 60_000);
        assert!(skipped["command"].as_str().unwrap().starts_with("snapback4 run record '{"));
    }
    let reset = host.post("/dev/reset", json!({"clock":"reset","seed":[{"table":"drivers","row":{"id":"one","n":0}}]}));
    assert!(reset["now"].is_i64() && reset["skewMs"].as_i64().unwrap().abs() < 1000, "{reset}");
    let advance = output_json(f.command(&["clock", "advance", "3m", "--each-boundary", "--port", port]));
    let mut expected = Vec::new();
    for schedule in advance["schedules"].as_array().unwrap() {
        assert_eq!(schedule["skippedBoundaries"], json!([]));
        for window in schedule["firedWindows"].as_array().unwrap() { expected.push(window["periodEnd"].as_i64().unwrap()); }
    }
    expected.sort();
    assert!(expected.len() >= 4);
    let rows = host.query("windows");
    let rows = rows.as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(rows.iter().map(|r| r["end"].as_i64().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), expected);
    assert!(rows.iter().all(|r| r["at"].as_i64().unwrap() >= advance["now"].as_i64().unwrap()));
    let status = host.request("GET", "/dev/clock/status", json!({}));
    assert!(status["now"].is_i64() && status["skewMs"].is_i64(), "{status}");
    let before = host.query("windows");
    let refused = f.command(&["clock", "advance", "10001m", "--each-boundary", "--port", port]);
    assert!(!refused.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&refused.stderr).contains("10000 schedule windows"));
    assert_eq!(host.query("windows"), before, "preflight refuses before admitting any window");
}

#[test]
fn kit3_first_http_query_materializes_only_the_live_dependencies_it_encounters() {
    let f = Fixture::new("kit3-opening");
    let source = "use personas alice\ntable announcements:\n  opens: time\n  until: time ?\n  note: text <=40\n  not before .opens\n  expire at .until\n  public 'test'\n  online only\nquery all():\n  return announcements first 100 by .id\nquery clock():\n  return now\n";
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), source).unwrap();
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
    let now = host.query("clock").as_i64().unwrap();
    let mut seed = Vec::new();
    for n in 0..20 { seed.push(json!({"table":"announcements","row":{"id":format!("row{n:02}"),"opens":now+1000,"until":null,"note":"revealed"}})); }
    seed.push(json!({"table":"announcements","row":{"id":"never","opens":now+1000,"until":now+500,"note":"must stay private"}}));
    let seeded = host.post("/seed", json!(seed)); assert!(seeded.get("denied").is_none(), "{seeded}");
    host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"2s"}));
    let reply = host.post("/q/all", json!({"args":{}}));
    assert_eq!(reply["complete"], true, "{reply}");
    assert_eq!(reply["data"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 20);
    assert!(reply.get("loading").is_none() && reply.get("pending_opening").is_none(), "{reply}");
    assert!(!reply.to_string().contains("must stay private"));
}

#[test]
fn kit3_each_boundary_action_deadlines_start_at_admission() {
    let f = Fixture::new("kit3-actions");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use server\nuse personas alice\naction ping(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time) deadline 1m <- viewer = 'service:clock'\nschedule pulse every 1m as service:clock: ping\n").unwrap();
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
    let advanced = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"5m","eachBoundary":true}));
    assert_eq!(advanced["schedules"][0]["firedWindows"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 5, "{advanced}");
    let store = rusqlite::Connection::open(f.0.join(".snapback4/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
    let (count, failed, earliest): (i64, i64, i64) = store.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*), SUM(state = 'failed'), MIN(deadline) FROM _requests", [], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?, row.get(2)?))).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(count, 5); assert_eq!(failed, 0);
    assert!(earliest >= advanced["now"].as_i64().unwrap() + 60_000);
}

#[test]
fn kit3_large_clock_report_uses_complete_http_and_runnable_no_argument_commands() {
    let f = Fixture::new("kit3-large-report");
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
    let port = host.address.rsplit(':').next().unwrap();
    let output = f.command(&["clock", "advance", "2000m", "--port", port]);
    assert!(output.stdout.len() > 65_536, "fixture must exercise the former transport ceiling");
    let reply = output_json(output);
    assert_eq!(reply["advancedMs"], 120_000_000);
    let skipped = reply["schedules"][0]["skippedBoundaries"].as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(skipped.len(), 999);
    assert_eq!(skipped[0]["command"], "snapback4 run tick '{}'");
    let replay = f.command(&["run", "tick", "{}"]);
    assert!(replay.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&replay.stderr));
}

#[test]
fn kit3_http_transitive_opening_dependencies_ignore_never_live_worlds() {
    let mut paired = Vec::new();
    for never_live in [false, true] {
        let f = Fixture::new(if never_live {"kit3-transitive-never"} else {"kit3-transitive-absent"});
        fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\ntable grants:\n  ticket: int\n  opens: time\n  until: time ?\n  unique ticket\n  not before .opens\n  expire at .until\n  public 'scheduled grant'\n  online only\ntable tasks:\n  ticket: int\n  read <- exists grants[ticket = .ticket]\n  online only\nquery all():\n  return tasks first 100 by .id\nquery clock():\n  return now\n").unwrap();
        let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
        let now = host.query("clock").as_i64().unwrap();
        let mut seed = Vec::new();
        for n in 0..20 {
            seed.push(json!({"table":"grants","row":{"id":format!("g{n:02}"),"ticket":n,"opens":now+1000,"until":null}}));
            seed.push(json!({"table":"tasks","row":{"id":format!("t{n:02}"),"ticket":n}}));
        }
        if never_live { seed.push(json!({"table":"grants","row":{"id":"secret-never","ticket":99,"opens":now+1000,"until":now+500}})); }
        let seeded = host.post("/seed", json!(seed)); assert!(seeded.get("denied").is_none(), "{seeded}");
        host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"2s"}));
        let reply = host.post("/q/all", json!({"args":{}}));
        assert!(reply.get("loading").is_none() && reply.get("denied").is_none(), "{reply}");
        assert_eq!(reply["data"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 20);
        paired.push(json!({"data":reply["data"],"complete":reply["complete"],"meter":reply["meter"]}));
    }
    assert_eq!(paired[0], paired[1], "never-live occupants change neither answer nor query meter");
}

#[test]
fn kit3_retained_search_overlap_waits_for_the_same_pending_publication() {
    let f = Fixture::new("kit3-retained-opening");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\ntable members:\n  room: id\n  principal: principal\n  unique room, principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer\n  sync to .principal\ntable notes:\n  room: id\n  opens: time\n  at: time\n  body: text <=100\n  search body\n  by byOpening: opens\n  not before .opens\n  read <- exists members[.room, viewer]\n  sync to members[.room] last 100 by .at\n  retain delivered-history\nquery search(q: text <=100, c: cursor ?):\n  return notes matching q first 10 by .at after c\nquery clock():\n  return now\n").unwrap();
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
    let now = host.query("clock").as_i64().unwrap();
    let mut rows: Vec<_> = (0..8).map(|n| json!({"table":"notes","row":{"id":format!("n{n}"),"room":"r","at":n,"opens":now+1000,"body":"coffee"}})).collect();
    rows.push(json!({"table":"members","row":{"id":"member","room":"r","principal":"dev:alice"}}));
    let seeded = host.post("/seed", json!(rows)); assert!(seeded.get("denied").is_none(), "{seeded}");
    host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"2s"}));
    let reply = host.post("/q/search", json!({"args":{"q":"coffee","c":null},"searchRetained":["n7"]}));
    assert!(reply.get("denied").is_none() && reply.get("loading").is_none(), "{reply}");
    assert_eq!(reply["data"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 8, "{reply}");
    assert_eq!(reply["searchCurrent"], json!(["n7"]));
}

#[test]
fn kit3_held_schedules_still_preflight_report_size_without_releasing_the_hold() {
    let f = Fixture::new("kit3-held-report");
    let mut source = SOURCE.replace("schedule ticking every 2m: tick\n", "");
    for n in 0..4200 { source.push_str(&format!("schedule held{n} every 2m: tick\n")); }
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), source).unwrap();
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
    let now = host.query("time").as_i64().unwrap();
    let created = host.post("/m/counter", json!({"args":{}})); assert_eq!(created["state"], "sent");
    let refused = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"1h","eachBoundary":true}));
    assert_eq!(refused["denied"]["code"], "E_CLOCK_INPUT", "{refused}");
    assert!(refused["denied"]["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("byte budget"));
    assert!(host.query("time").as_i64().unwrap() < now + 60_000, "failed preflight must not advance time");
    assert_eq!(host.query("counters")[0]["n"], 0, "the hold and queued state stay unchanged");
}

#[test]
fn kit3_clock_reply_and_preflight_use_the_persisted_authoritative_floor() {
    let f = Fixture::new("kit3-reply-floor");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\ntable timed:\n  opens: time\n  not before .opens\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nquery clock():\n  return now\n").unwrap();
    let old = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--clock-offset", "2h"]);
    let future = old.query("clock").as_i64().unwrap();
    drop(old);
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
    let before = host.query("clock").as_i64().unwrap(); assert!(before >= future);
    let advanced = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"1m"}));
    assert!(advanced["now"].as_i64().unwrap() >= before, "{advanced}");
    let actual = host.query("clock").as_i64().unwrap();
    assert!((actual - advanced["now"].as_i64().unwrap()).abs() < 1000, "{advanced}; actual={actual}");
    assert!(advanced["skewMs"].as_i64().unwrap() > 7_000_000, "{advanced}");
}

#[test]
fn kit4_thirty_day_coalesced_advance_has_no_window_cap() {
    let f = Fixture::new("kit4-long-jump");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), SOURCE.replace("every 2m:", "every 5m:")).unwrap();
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--memory", "--no-watch"]);
    host.post("/m/counter", json!({"args":{}}));
    let refused = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"40d", "eachBoundary":true}));
    assert!(refused["denied"]["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("omit --each-boundary"), "{refused}");
    let reply = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"30d"}));
    assert!(reply.get("denied").is_none(), "{reply}");
    let schedule = &reply["schedules"][0];
    assert_eq!(schedule["firedWindows"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(schedule["skippedPeriods"], 8639);
    assert_eq!(schedule["skippedBoundaries"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 32);
    assert_eq!(schedule["skippedBoundariesOmitted"], 8607);
    assert_eq!(host.query("counters")[0]["n"], 1);
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! Bounded, read-only evidence from the store's own commit clock.
use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

const SKIPPED_PREVIEW: usize = 32;
const TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 5_000;
const RECENT_COMMITS: usize = 64;
const TABLE_BYTES: usize = 4096;
const TABLE_NAMES: usize = 8;
const REPORT_ERROR_BYTES: usize = 1024;
// Reserve one MiB for the outer clock/work envelope inside the host encoder.
const REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET: usize = crate::serve::JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING - (1 << 20);

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — inspect a fixed recent commit window, never application row values.
pub fn ahead(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Result<Option<String>, RuntimeError> {
    // Reverse primary-key traversal: no full scan, sort, row payloads or retained
    // historical stamp set. octet_length(column) uses SQLite record metadata;
    // unlike a text length/cast/substr, it need not load oversized payloads.
    let mut query = engine.store().conn().prepare(
        "SELECT at, CASE WHEN at > ?1 AND octet_length(touched) <= ?2 THEN CAST(touched AS BLOB) END,
         octet_length(touched) FROM _commits ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT ?3")?;
    let mut rows = query.query(rusqlite::params![now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS), TABLE_BYTES as i64, RECENT_COMMITS as i64])?;
    let mut lead = 0;
    let mut tables = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut omitted = false;
    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
        let at: i64 = row.get(0)?;
        if at <= now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS) { continue; }
        lead = lead.max(at.saturating_sub(now));
        let length: i64 = row.get(2)?;
        if length > TABLE_BYTES as i64 { omitted = true; continue; }
        let bytes: Vec<u8> = row.get(1)?;
        match serde_json::from_slice::<Vec<String>>(&bytes) {
            Ok(names) => for name in names {
                if tables.contains(&name) { continue; }
                if tables.len() < TABLE_NAMES && name.len() <= 128 { tables.insert(name); }
                else { omitted = true; }
            },
            Err(_) => omitted = true,
        }
    }
    if lead == 0 { return Ok(None); }
    let mut names = tables.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if omitted { names.push("additional tables omitted".into()); }
    if names.is_empty() { names.push("no table names recorded".into()); }
    Ok(Some(format!(
        "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD: recent commit stamps lead the current clock by up to {lead}ms (latest {RECENT_COMMITS} commits; tables: {}); use a fresh store per offset test ({}), or restart with the same --clock-offset (or dev --resume-clock) to continue",
        names.join(", "), crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())))
}

/// UTC calendar text for Unix milliseconds: schedule boundaries are UTC.
pub fn utc(ms: i64) -> String {
    let (days, rem) = (ms.div_euclid(86_400_000), ms.rem_euclid(86_400_000));
    // Civil-from-days (proleptic Gregorian), exact for the I53 range.
    let z = days + 719_468;
    let era = z.div_euclid(146_097);
    let doe = z - era * 146_097;
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1_460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let day = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let month = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 };
    let year = yoe + era * 400 + i64::from(month <= 2);
    format!("{year:04}-{month:02}-{day:02}T{:02}:{:02}:{:02}Z", rem / 3_600_000, rem % 3_600_000 / 60_000, rem % 60_000 / 1_000)
}

/// A positive duration in the units `clock advance` accepts, rounded up to a second.
pub fn span(ms: i64) -> String {
    let seconds = (ms.max(0) + 999) / 1_000;
    let (d, h, m, s) = (seconds / 86_400, seconds % 86_400 / 3_600, seconds % 3_600 / 60, seconds % 60);
    let parts: Vec<String> = [(d, "d"), (h, "h"), (m, "m"), (s, "s")].into_iter().filter(|(n, _)| *n > 0).map(|(n, unit)| format!("{n}{unit}")).collect();
    if parts.is_empty() { "0s".into() } else { parts.join("") }
}

/// Each schedule's saved boundary, in declaration order.
pub fn saved_boundaries(engine: &Engine) -> Vec<Option<i64>> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter()
        .map(|schedule| {
            let shape = engine.store().meta(&format!("schedule-shape:{}", schedule.name)).ok().flatten();
            if shape.as_ref().is_some_and(|shape| *shape != snapback4_core::value::digest(schedule)) { return None; }
            engine.store().meta(&format!("schedule:{}", schedule.name)).ok().flatten().and_then(|value| value.parse().ok())
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Preflight is bounded before the clock or worker leases change.
// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.1 — only explicit execution enumerates all crossed windows; coalesced reports keep a bounded preview.
pub fn crossed_boundaries(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool) -> Result<Vec<(usize, i64)>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    let mut boundaries = Vec::new();
    let mut report_bytes = 0usize;
    for (index, schedule) in engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate() {
        // JSON may escape every error byte; count that bound per schedule.
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add(2048 + 6 * (REPORT_ERROR_BYTES + 3) + 8 * (schedule.name.len() + schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule report exceeds its byte budget; reduce the declared schedules")); }
        if held { continue; }
        let Some(old) = before.get(index).copied().flatten() else { continue; };
        let end = schedule.boundary(now);
        if old >= end { continue; }
        let periods = schedule.periods_between(old, end);
        if each_boundary && periods > (10_000 - boundaries.len()) as i64 {
            return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("--each-boundary crosses more than 10000 schedule windows; use a smaller duration or omit --each-boundary to coalesce"));
        }
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add((if each_boundary { periods as usize } else { (periods as usize).min(SKIPPED_PREVIEW + 1) }).saturating_mul(1024 + 8 * schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule window report exceeds its byte budget; use a smaller duration")); }
        if !each_boundary { continue; }
        let mut boundary = schedule.next(old);
        while boundary <= end {
            boundaries.push((index, boundary));
            boundary = schedule.next(boundary);
        }
    }
    boundaries.sort_by_key(|(index, at)| (*at, *index));
    Ok(boundaries)
}

fn period(schedule: &snapback4_core::schema::Schedule) -> String {
    schedule.describe()
}

/// What each schedule did on one clock advance, and why one did not fire.
/// The core fires each schedule at most once per advance, for the latest
/// boundary, and holds it while its saved boundary is ahead of the clock.
// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — missed boundaries fire once; this only explains them.
pub fn schedule_report(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], fired: &[String], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
    let after = saved_boundaries(engine);
    // Read only bounded summaries: job args/cursors and full error payloads
    // are irrelevant to this report and must not be loaded then replicated.
    let runs = job_summaries(engine).unwrap_or_default();
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, schedule)| {
        let (old, saved) = (before.get(index).copied().flatten(), after.get(index).copied().flatten());
        let current = schedule.boundary(now);
        let next = schedule.next(current);
        let mut entry = serde_json::json!({ "name": schedule.name, "target": schedule.target, "period": period(schedule), "fired": false, "firedWindows": [], "skippedBoundaries": [], "nextBoundary": utc(next) });
        if fired.contains(&schedule.name) {
            let end = saved.unwrap_or(current);
            entry["fired"] = true.into();
            let start = schedule.previous(end);
            let window = |boundary| serde_json::json!({"periodStart": schedule.previous(boundary), "periodEnd": boundary});
            let mut windows = Vec::new();
            if each_boundary {
                if let Some(old) = old {
                    let mut at = schedule.next(old);
                    while at <= end { windows.push(window(at)); at = schedule.next(at); }
                }
            } else { windows.push(window(end)); }
            entry["firedWindows"] = windows.into();
            entry["periodStart"] = start.into();
            entry["periodEnd"] = end.into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("fired for the period {} to {}", utc(start), utc(end)).into();
            if let Some(run) = runs.get(&schedule.target) { entry["run"] = run.clone(); }
            // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — calendar periods count boundaries, not milliseconds.
            let skipped = if each_boundary { 0 } else { old.map(|old| schedule.periods_between(old, end) - 1).unwrap_or(0) };
            if skipped > 0 {
                entry["skippedPeriods"] = skipped.into();
                let mut windows = Vec::new();
                let mut at = schedule.next(old.unwrap());
                while at < end && windows.len() < SKIPPED_PREVIEW {
                    let mut item = window(at);
                    let mut args = serde_json::Map::new();
                    if let Some(program) = engine.program(&schedule.target) {
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") { args.insert("periodStart".into(), schedule.previous(at).into()); }
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") { args.insert("periodEnd".into(), at.into()); }
                    }
                    item["command"] = format!("snapback4 run {} '{}'", schedule.target, serde_json::Value::Object(args)).into();
                    windows.push(item); at = schedule.next(at);
                }
                entry["skippedBoundariesOmitted"] = (skipped - windows.len() as i64).into();
                entry["skippedBoundaries"] = windows.into();
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired once, for the latest period {} to {}; {skipped} earlier period{} this advance crossed (from {}) did not run: missed boundaries fire once. Run {} with periodStart/periodEnd for an earlier period",
                    utc(start), utc(end), if skipped == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, utc(old.unwrap_or(end)), schedule.target).into();
            }
        } else if held {
            entry["reason"] = "schedules are held (dev --hold-schedules or --replay); a restart without the flag resumes at the next boundary".into();
        } else if let Some(saved) = saved.filter(|saved| *saved > current) {
            let due = schedule.next(saved);
            entry["heldUntil"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["nextBoundary"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("held by the store: its saved boundary {} is ahead of the clock {} (a dev restart resets the clock offset; the store keeps the boundary). Advance {} more, restart with dev --resume-clock, or test on dev --fresh",
                utc(saved), utc(now), span(due - now)).into();
        } else if old != saved {
            entry["reason"] = format!("{} started at boundary {}: a schedule fires at the boundary after the first one it sees, next {} ({} from now)",
                if old.is_none() { "this schedule" } else { "this changed schedule" }, utc(saved.unwrap_or(current)), utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        } else {
            entry["reason"] = format!("next boundary {} ({} from now; schedule times are UTC)", utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        }
        entry
    }).collect()
}

/// The report never loads full error/argument payloads from retained jobs.
fn job_summaries(engine: &Engine) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, serde_json::Value>, RuntimeError> {
    let mut statement = engine.store().conn().prepare(
        "SELECT name, state, rows_written, substr(error, 1, ?1), octet_length(error) > ?1, id FROM _jobs ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 64")?;
    let mut rows = statement.query([REPORT_ERROR_BYTES as i64])?;
    let mut summaries = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
        let name: String = row.get(0)?;
        if summaries.contains_key(&name) { continue; }
        let mut error: Option<String> = row.get(3)?;
        let truncated = row.get::<_, Option<bool>>(4)?.unwrap_or(false);
        if let Some(text) = &mut error && truncated {
            let mut end = REPORT_ERROR_BYTES.min(text.len());
            while !text.is_char_boundary(end) { end -= 1; }
            text.truncate(end); text.push('…');
        }
        summaries.insert(name, serde_json::json!({ "id":row.get::<_, String>(5)?, "state":row.get::<_, String>(1)?,
            "rowsWritten":row.get::<_, i64>(2)?, "error":error, "errorTruncated":truncated }));
    }
    Ok(summaries)
}

/// Startup lines for schedules the store holds: their saved boundary leads the clock.
pub fn held_schedules(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Vec<String> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().zip(saved_boundaries(engine)).filter_map(|(schedule, saved)| {
        let saved = saved.filter(|saved| *saved > schedule.boundary(now))?;
        let due = schedule.next(saved);
        Some(format!("clock     schedule {} is held until {} ({} from now): its saved boundary {} is ahead of this clock; `clock advance {}` or restart with dev --resume-clock",
            schedule.name, utc(due), span(due - now), utc(saved), span(due - now)))
    }).collect()
}

/// The dev clock offset that continues a store's own time: its newest
/// recent commit stamp (or saved schedule boundary) less wall time, never negative.
pub fn resume_offset(store: &snapback4_core::Store, wall: i64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    let commit: Option<i64> = store.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT MAX(at) FROM (SELECT at FROM _commits ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT ?1)", [RECENT_COMMITS as i64], |row| row.get(0))?;
    let mut statement = store.conn().prepare("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key LIKE 'schedule:%'")?;
    let boundary = statement.query_map([], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0))?.flatten().filter_map(|value| value.parse::<i64>().ok()).max();
    let latest = commit.into_iter().chain(boundary).max().unwrap_or(wall);
    // Whole seconds, rounded up, so restarting twice does not drift backwards.
    Ok((latest.saturating_sub(wall).max(0) + 999) / 1_000 * 1_000)
}

pub fn has_data(engine: &Engine) -> bool {
    engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r)", [], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap_or(false)
}

pub fn offset_line(offset: i64, running: bool) -> String {
    format!("clock     offset from wall time: {offset:+}ms{}", if running { " (lasts until restart)" } else { " (no running dev; inspecting against wall time)" })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn engine() -> Engine {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table alpha:\n  at: time\n  public\ntable beta:\n  at: time\n  public\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program), 100_000).unwrap()
    }
    fn seed(engine: &mut Engine, table: &str, commit_at: i64, row_at: i64) {
        engine.seed_at([(table.into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
            ("id", snapback4_core::Value::Id(snapback4_core::ids::mint(row_at, &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).unwrap())),
            ("at", snapback4_core::Value::Int(row_at)),
        ]))], commit_at).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn clock_report_bounds_one_large_job_error_repeated_across_many_schedules() {
        let mut source = "table rows:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\njob tick():\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row.id] {n:1}\n".to_owned();
        for n in 0..500 { source.push_str(&format!("schedule s{n} every 1m: tick\n")); }
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine = Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program), 0).unwrap();
        engine.fire_schedules(0).unwrap();
        let before = saved_boundaries(&engine);
        assert_eq!(crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, 60_000, false, true).unwrap().len(), 500);
        let fired = engine.fire_schedules(60_000).unwrap();
        let message = "🧪\n\\".repeat(30_000);
        engine.store().conn().execute("UPDATE _jobs SET error = ?1 WHERE id = (SELECT id FROM _jobs ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1)", [&message]).unwrap();
        let report = schedule_report(&engine, &before, &fired, 60_000, false, false);
        assert_eq!(report.len(), 500);
        assert!(report.iter().all(|s| s["run"]["errorTruncated"] == true));
        assert!(report.iter().all(|s| s["run"]["error"].as_str().unwrap().len() <= REPORT_ERROR_BYTES + 3));
        assert!(serde_json::to_vec(&report).unwrap().len() < 2 << 20);
    }
    #[test]
    fn clock_report_refuses_before_the_host_encoder_limit() {
        let mut source = "table rows:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\njob tick():\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row.id] {n:1}\n".to_owned();
        for n in 0..3000 { source.push_str(&format!("schedule s{n} every 1m: tick\n")); }
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine = Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program), 0).unwrap();
        engine.fire_schedules(0).unwrap();
        let before = saved_boundaries(&engine);
        let refusal = crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, 60_000, false, true).unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(refusal.code, "E_CLOCK_INPUT");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("byte budget"));
        assert_eq!(before, saved_boundaries(&engine));
        assert!(REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET < crate::serve::JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING);
    }
    #[test]
    fn utc_text_and_spans_match_the_calendar() {
        assert_eq!(utc(0), "1970-01-01T00:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(951_782_400_000), "2000-02-29T00:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(-1), "1969-12-31T23:59:59Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(1_791_468_000_000), "2026-10-08T14:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(span(12_633_000), "3h30m33s");
        assert_eq!(span(86_400_001), "1d1s");
        assert_eq!(span(0), "0s");
    }
    #[test]
    fn resume_offset_follows_the_newest_commit_and_is_never_negative() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 100_000).unwrap(), 0);
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 3_700_500, 1);
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 100_000).unwrap(), 3_601_000);
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 9_000_000).unwrap(), 0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn commit_tolerance_and_largest_lead_ignore_future_application_values() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 100_000, 1_000_000);
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().is_none());
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 105_000, 2_000_000);
        seed(&mut engine, "beta", 107_000, 3_000_000);
        let note = ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("up to 7000ms (latest 64 commits; tables: beta)"), "{note}");
        let note = ahead(&engine, 99_999).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("up to 7001ms (latest 64 commits; tables: alpha, beta)"), "{note}");
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 102_000).unwrap().is_none());
    }
    #[test]
    fn recent_window_does_not_claim_older_commits_or_read_row_payloads() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 200_000, 200_000);
        for n in 0..RECENT_COMMITS { seed(&mut engine, "beta", 100_000, 300_000 + n as i64); }
        // Deliberately unreadable images: metadata inspection must not deserialize them.
        engine.store().conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data = 'not json'", []).unwrap();
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().is_none());
        engine.store().conn().execute("UPDATE _commits SET at = 200000, touched = ?1 WHERE seq = (SELECT MAX(seq) FROM _commits)", ["x".repeat(TABLE_BYTES + 1)]).unwrap();
        let note = ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("100000ms") && note.contains("additional tables omitted"), "{note}");
    }
}
