### 20.12.1 Wave 14 development clock windows (KIT3, 2026-10-16)

Implementer: Codex, 2026-10-16. The ordinary scheduler and default clock advance
keep latest-window coalescing: production must not replay an unbounded backlog
of effects after downtime. Development tests can request `clock advance
--each-boundary` (HTTP `eachBoundary:true`) to admit every missed window in
chronological boundary order, with declaration order breaking ties. Jobs drain
in that order on the final advanced clock; their periodStart/periodEnd arguments
name historical windows, while `now` remains the final advanced time. This is backlog admission, not
a historical simulation: test period-dependent work using periodStart/periodEnd,
not now. To test job logic dependent on now at each boundary, issue separate
advances. Scheduled
actions are admitted in order at the final advanced time (their deadlines
begin at admission, never at a historical periodEnd); external workers retain
their normal async execution contract. First-seen/changed schedules only prime their current
boundary. Held schedules remain held. Boundary/job admission keeps the existing
atomic saved-boundary transaction; restart never replays an admitted boundary.

Preflight always validates report metadata, including with held/replay schedules;
the hold only suppresses boundary enumeration/admission. Under --each-boundary it enumerates at most 10,000 crossed windows in total across all schedules, before
moving time or leases. A conservative report-size estimate also refuses above
15 MiB, reserving 1 MiB for the outer clock/work envelope inside the host's
16 MiB JSON encoder (and the existing 64 MiB CLI HTTP exchange).
Retained job summaries project only bounded fields; error previews are capped
at 1024 UTF-8 bytes plus an ellipsis, with errorTruncated and the run id. The
preflight includes the worst JSON escaping cost for that preview per schedule;
full error/args/cursor payloads are never loaded into the report.
Larger requests refuse E_CLOCK_INPUT and advise a smaller
advance; this bounds both execution and the explicit report. Each schedule's
reply includes firedWindows and skippedBoundaries (periodStart, periodEnd and,
for each skipped job window, its literal `snapback4 run <job>` command).
Coalesced advances have no crossed-window cap: they execute only the latest window
per schedule, retain the exact skippedPeriods count, and preview the first 32
skippedBoundaries per schedule with skippedBoundariesOmitted naming the remainder.
Report preflight budgets that bounded preview independently of jump distance.
Calendar schedules use calendar previous/next, never fixed-duration division.
An incomplete drain reports the already advanced now/skewMs and a refusal;
shutdown specifically refuses non-retryable E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED rather than
turning a stopped job/delivery loop into successful HTTP 200;
callers inspect it and never blindly retry an advance. Ordinary maintenance
continues queued work; `run <job>` can inspect a run and `reset --clock reset`
provides an explicit fresh-test recovery if the scenario should be restarted. The default retains
existing fired/periodStart/periodEnd fields and adds the explicit windows.

Successful dev reset, clock status and clock advance replies include `now` and
`skewMs` (served time minus sampled wall time). The existing durable timed-
disclosure floor participates in both reported now and window preflight; they
cannot describe an earlier raw process-clock value after a persisted restart.
This reports the existing authoritative floor without changing its lifetime law.
Existing offset fields remain.
Reset keeps its current time unless clock:reset was requested. Error replies
after a clock change include the same clock evidence. No serve time controls,
clock rewind, job authority or production scheduler change is introduced.

# Rules

**Snapback 2 optimizes the edit-to-verified loop — the agent's building an app on it, and ours building it.**

Speed is the constraint. Everything below exists to keep the edit-to-verified loop short
and to stop this repository accreting apparatus faster than anyone can remove it. These
rules bind `snapback2/` and the LLPs numbered 1000 and up; Snapback 1 is baselined, not
exempt, and its numbers may only fall.

Each rule is **[check]** (enforced by `caps`) or **[review]** (enforced by a human).

## Budgets — every budget is a trade, not a limit

- **5 blocking checks, 60s total.** A sixth requires deleting one, in the same PR. **[check]**
- **59 documents in the working set** (`llp/current/`). Today's count. A 60th requires
  archiving one — which is just removing the link. Snapback 2 aims to leave this at 15;
  Snapback 1's links are archived at promotion. **[check]**
- **12 documents in the foundation** (`llp/foundation/`). **[check]**
- **This file: 700 words.** If it grows, something becomes a check or stops being a rule. **[check]**
- **1,500 lines per source file, baseline 204.** Generated files exempt — and generated
  files are built, not committed; so are benchmark run records under `benchmarks/runs/`,
  which are evidence, not source. The baseline is Snapback 1's oversize count on the day
  the rule landed; it may not grow, and `snapback2/` contributes zero to it. **[check]**

## Blocking checks

`caps` · `ref-check` · `snapback2/scripts/test.sh` · `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs --check` · `check-inventories`

## Loop shape

- **Every check reports all failures in one run.** Fail-fast is banned. **[check]**
- **Nothing blocks on anything slower than 60 seconds.** Slow verification runs on a
  schedule, attributed to the breaking commit, and files a bounded set of issues. **[review]**
- **Fix loops get 3 rounds.** Then stop: escalate to a human or descope. **[review]**

## Time budgets

Tracked every run of `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs` (appends `snapback2/metrics/loop.jsonl`).
A regression is a P0 with a name on it.

| | budget |
|---|---|
| Incremental rebuild after a one-line edit | 15s |
| Test what you just changed | 30s |
| Blocking gate suite | 60s |
| Full build, warm cache | 5 min |
| **`snapback2 check` on a program (the agent's product latency)** | **1s** |
| **`snapback2 doctor` boot-to-ready** | **0.5s** |

## Scope

- **`NOT-DOING.md` is binding.** Moving something onto the doing-list means writing why
  and taking something off. **[review]**
- **Delete; don't deprecate.** No compat shims or migration paths before promotion. **[review]**
- **Nothing is compiled, transpiled, or generated at runtime that could be built ahead of
  time.** **[review]**
- **A spec needs an implementer and a date, or it isn't written.** **[review]**
- **Only implemented specs bind.** A document with no running code behind it is a
  proposal, and a proposal cannot block a PR or justify a check. **[review]**

## Agents

- **Agents remove apparatus freely and add none.** An agent PR that adds a check, script,
  registry, config file, or design doc needs explicit human approval saying so. **[review]**
- **2026-09-06 session exception:** the user delegated the search implementation
  decisions; one `snapback2-core/build.rs` may generate pinned Unicode tables at
  build time, alongside the existing package build. No new gate, registry,
  runtime generator, or Unicode dependency is authorized by this exception. **[review]**
- **Snapback 2 LLPs are numbered 1000 and up**; `llp-create` must be told. **[review]**
- **Snapback 4 LLPs are numbered 3000 and up** (`snapback4/`, LLP 3000); it adopts these budgets
  and loop shape as its own and keeps its scope in `snapback4/NOT-DOING.md`. **[review]**

## Shared machines

- Warm, named lanes, one per stream of work. Never clone fresh; reuse the worktree.
- **Never `git stash`.** Use a temporary commit.
- Kill only PIDs you recorded at launch. Never by name or pattern.

 # Snapback 4 behavior changes
 
+## 0.4.32
+
+- Clock advances coalesce arbitrarily many crossed windows; the 10,000-window cap applies only to `--each-boundary`. Skipped-window replies retain exact counts and preview up to 32 replay commands per schedule.
+
 ## 0.4.31
 
 - Existing stores keep their rows, ids, schema and captured `read through` authority. No automatic data repair or audience expansion occurs. Regenerate declarations and rebuild web, native and Exact clients to pick up the runtime and client changes below; review new correctness notes before deploying edited schemas.

/// What each schedule did on one clock advance, and why one did not fire.
/// The core fires each schedule at most once per advance, for the latest
/// boundary, and holds it while its saved boundary is ahead of the clock.
// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — missed boundaries fire once; this only explains them.
pub fn schedule_report(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], fired: &[String], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
    let after = saved_boundaries(engine);
    // Read only bounded summaries: job args/cursors and full error payloads
    // are irrelevant to this report and must not be loaded then replicated.
    let runs = job_summaries(engine).unwrap_or_default();
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, schedule)| {
        let (old, saved) = (before.get(index).copied().flatten(), after.get(index).copied().flatten());
        let current = schedule.boundary(now);
        let next = schedule.next(current);
        let mut entry = serde_json::json!({ "name": schedule.name, "target": schedule.target, "period": period(schedule), "fired": false, "firedWindows": [], "skippedBoundaries": [], "nextBoundary": utc(next) });
        if fired.contains(&schedule.name) {
            let end = saved.unwrap_or(current);
            entry["fired"] = true.into();
            let start = schedule.previous(end);
            let window = |boundary| serde_json::json!({"periodStart": schedule.previous(boundary), "periodEnd": boundary});
            let mut windows = Vec::new();
            if each_boundary {
                if let Some(old) = old {
                    let mut at = schedule.next(old);
                    while at <= end { windows.push(window(at)); at = schedule.next(at); }
                }
            } else { windows.push(window(end)); }
            entry["firedWindows"] = windows.into();
            entry["periodStart"] = start.into();
            entry["periodEnd"] = end.into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("fired for the period {} to {}", utc(start), utc(end)).into();
            if let Some(run) = runs.get(&schedule.target) { entry["run"] = run.clone(); }
            // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — calendar periods count boundaries, not milliseconds.
            let skipped = if each_boundary { 0 } else { old.map(|old| schedule.periods_between(old, end) - 1).unwrap_or(0) };
            if skipped > 0 {
                entry["skippedPeriods"] = skipped.into();
                let mut windows = Vec::new();
                let mut at = schedule.next(old.unwrap());
                while at < end && windows.len() < SKIPPED_PREVIEW {
                    let mut item = window(at);
                    let mut args = serde_json::Map::new();
                    if let Some(program) = engine.program(&schedule.target) {
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") { args.insert("periodStart".into(), schedule.previous(at).into()); }
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") { args.insert("periodEnd".into(), at.into()); }
                    }
                    item["command"] = format!("snapback4 run {} '{}'", schedule.target, serde_json::Value::Object(args)).into();
                    windows.push(item); at = schedule.next(at);
                }
                entry["skippedBoundariesOmitted"] = (skipped - windows.len() as i64).into();
                entry["skippedBoundaries"] = windows.into();
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired once, for the latest period {} to {}; {skipped} earlier period{} this advance crossed (from {}) did not run: missed boundaries fire once. Run {} with periodStart/periodEnd for an earlier period",
                    utc(start), utc(end), if skipped == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, utc(old.unwrap_or(end)), schedule.target).into();
            }
        } else if held {
            entry["reason"] = "schedules are held (dev --hold-schedules or --replay); a restart without the flag resumes at the next boundary".into();
        } else if let Some(saved) = saved.filter(|saved| *saved > current) {
            let due = schedule.next(saved);
            entry["heldUntil"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["nextBoundary"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("held by the store: its saved boundary {} is ahead of the clock {} (a dev restart resets the clock offset; the store keeps the boundary). Advance {} more, restart with dev --resume-clock, or test on dev --fresh",
                utc(saved), utc(now), span(due - now)).into();
        } else if old != saved {
            entry["reason"] = format!("{} started at boundary {}: a schedule fires at the boundary after the first one it sees, next {} ({} from now)",
                if old.is_none() { "this schedule" } else { "this changed schedule" }, utc(saved.unwrap_or(current)), utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        } else {
            entry["reason"] = format!("next boundary {} ({} from now; schedule times are UTC)", utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        }
        entry
    }).collect()
}

/// The report never loads full error/argument payloads from retained jobs.
fn job_summaries(engine: &Engine) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, serde_json::Value>, RuntimeError> {
    let mut statement = engine.store().conn().prepare(
        "SELECT name, state, rows_written, substr(error, 1, ?1), octet_length(error) > ?1, id FROM _jobs ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 64")?;
    let mut rows = statement.query([REPORT_ERROR_BYTES as i64])?;
    let mut summaries = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
        let name: String = row.get(0)?;
        if summaries.contains_key(&name) { continue; }
        let mut error: Option<String> = row.get(3)?;
        let truncated = row.get::<_, Option<bool>>(4)?.unwrap_or(false);
        if let Some(text) = &mut error && truncated {
            let mut end = REPORT_ERROR_BYTES.min(text.len());
            while !text.is_char_boundary(end) { end -= 1; }
            text.truncate(end); text.push('…');
        }
        summaries.insert(name, serde_json::json!({ "id":row.get::<_, String>(5)?, "state":row.get::<_, String>(1)?,
            "rowsWritten":row.get::<_, i64>(2)?, "error":error, "errorTruncated":truncated }));
    }
    Ok(summaries)
}

/// Startup lines for schedules the store holds: their saved boundary leads the clock.
pub fn held_schedules(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Vec<String> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().zip(saved_boundaries(engine)).filter_map(|(schedule, saved)| {
        let saved = saved.filter(|saved| *saved > schedule.boundary(now))?;
fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if stopping {
        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
    }
    Ok(work)
}

pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.personas { return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release())})); }
    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
    };
    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
        None => false,
        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
    };
    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
        .and_then(crate::dev_clock::duration) {
        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
    let advanced = (|| {
        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
        let mut next = clock.clone();
        let now = next.advance(milliseconds)?.max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire));
        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary)?;
        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
        engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
        *clock = next;
        let offset = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();
        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
        } else { String::new() };
        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (authoritative floor included); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
        Ok((now, offset, message, before, boundaries))
    })();
    let (now, offset, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
        Ok(result) => result,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
    let drained = (|| {
        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
        if each_boundary {
            for (index, at) in boundaries {
                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(now, index, at))?);
            }
        }
        let mut work = work(shared, true, 0)?;
        work.schedules += crossed.len();
        work.fired.extend(crossed);
        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
    })();
    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
        Ok(work) => {
            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary);
            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":offset, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules}))
        }
        // Time remains advanced if maintenance fails; callers must not
        // retry the advance blindly. The normal tick retries pending work.
        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":offset, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    }
}

/// `POST /dev/jobs/<job>` with `{"args":{..}}`: queue one run of a job as the
/// system on the dev clock and drive chunks until it is done or parked. This is
/// what `snapback4 run <job>` forwards to while dev owns the store.
pub(super) fn run_job(request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, body: &str) {
    use snapback4_core::{JobState, error::Refusal, ir::ProgramKind, wire};
    let service_route=request.url().starts_with("/jobs/");
    let service=if service_route {
        match super::session_identity::principal_of(&request,shared) {
            Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind=="service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Some(principal),
            _ => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","triggering a deployed system job requires a full service token").guide("jobs"))})),
        }
    } else {
        if !shared.personas {return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","/dev/jobs is a development door; use run <job> --release <dir> with a service token").guide("jobs"))}));}
        None
    };
    let refuse = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| respond(request, status, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal.guide("jobs"))}));
    let payload: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(if body.trim().is_empty() { "{}" } else { body }) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(error) => return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("the request body is not JSON: {error}; send {{\"args\":{{..}}}}"))),
    };
    if service_route && !shared.personas {crate::release_requests::Scope::admit(&shared.release_requests,||*lock(&shared.generation));}
    let program = lock(&shared.bound).programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == name).map(|p| p.program.clone());
    let program = match program {
        Some(program) if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job => program,
        Some(other) => return refuse(request, 404, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{name} is a {}, not a job; queries are POST /q/<op>, mutations POST /m/<op>", other.kind.word()))),
        None => return respond(request, 404, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&super::dev_doors::stale(shared, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("unknown job {name}")).guide("jobs")))})),
    };
    if let Some(raw)=payload.get("args") && let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&program.args,raw) {return refuse(request,400,why);}
    let mut args = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    if let Some(fields) = payload.get("args").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object) {
        for (field, value) in fields {
            let Some(kind) = program.args.get(field) else { return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{name} takes no argument {field}"))); };
            match wire::from_json(kind, value) {
                Ok(typed) => { args.insert(field.clone(), typed); }
                Err(why) => return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("argument {field}: {why}"))),
            }
        }
    }
    let mut admitted=lock(&shared.engine);
    crate::release_requests::stamp(admitted.generation());
    if let Some(service)=&service {
        if let Err(why)=super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request,shared,&admitted,service) {drop(admitted);return refuse(request,403,why);}
    }
    let queued=admitted.enqueue_job(name,args,shared.now());
    drop(admitted);
    crate::release_requests::executed();
    let id = match queued {
        Ok(id) => id,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
    };
    // FIFO: earlier queued runs finish first, as with `run` on a stopped store;
    // the background tick may run some chunks too, through the same engine.
    let run = loop {
        let current = {let engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.job(&id)};
        match current {
            Ok(Some(run)) if matches!(run.state, JobState::Done | JobState::Parked) || crate::lifecycle::stopping() => break run,
            Ok(Some(_)) => {}
            Ok(None) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&RuntimeError::Store(format!("job {id} vanished")).refusal())})),
            Err(error) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
        }
        let (chunk, logs) = engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| (engine.run_job_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget), engine.take_native_logs()));
        for line in logs { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: native {line}"); }
        match chunk {
            Ok(Some(chunk)) if chunk.state == JobState::Parked && shared.personas => shared.refusals.job(&chunk),
            Ok(_) => {}
            Err(error) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
        }
    };

//! Schedule boundaries: fixed periods (`every 2m`, `every 1d at 08:00`) and
//! UTC calendar months (`every 1 month at day 1 09:00`, `every 1 year`).
//! The scheduler, the dev clock and `doctor` use only these methods.
use super::Schedule;

pub(super) fn is_zero_months(months: &u32) -> bool { *months == 0 }

/// The latest offset a calendar schedule may name: the end of day 28.
pub const CALENDAR_OFFSET_LIMIT_MS: u64 = 28 * 86_400_000;
/// At most once a century.
pub const CALENDAR_MONTHS_LIMIT: u32 = 1_200;

impl Schedule {
    /// The most recent period boundary at or before `now`.
    pub fn boundary(&self, now: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 {
            let n = i64::from(self.months);
            let mut month = crate::calendar::month_of(now).div_euclid(n) * n;
            if self.month_boundary(month) > now { month -= n; }
            return self.month_boundary(month);
        }
        let every = self.every_ms as i64;
        let offset = self.offset_ms as i64;
        (now - offset).div_euclid(every) * every + offset
    }

    /// The boundary one period before `boundary`: the `periodStart` of the
    /// period that ends there.
    pub fn previous(&self, boundary: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 { return self.month_boundary(crate::calendar::month_of(boundary) - i64::from(self.months)); }
        boundary.saturating_sub(self.every_ms as i64)
    }

    /// The boundary one period after `boundary`.
    pub fn next(&self, boundary: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 { return self.month_boundary(crate::calendar::month_of(boundary) + i64::from(self.months)); }
        boundary.saturating_add(self.every_ms as i64)
    }

    /// How many boundaries lie in `(from, to]`, for two boundaries of this schedule.
    pub fn periods_between(&self, from: i64, to: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 { return (crate::calendar::month_of(to) - crate::calendar::month_of(from)).div_euclid(i64::from(self.months)); }
        (to - from) / (self.every_ms as i64).max(1)
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — exact: the month start plus the offset in i128,
    // never an endpoint substituted at the i53 edges (only ±285,000 years out).
    fn month_boundary(&self, month: i64) -> i64 {
        let days = crate::calendar::days_from_civil(1970 + i128::from(month).div_euclid(12), i128::from(month).rem_euclid(12) + 1, 1);
        let at = days * i128::from(crate::calendar::DAY_MS) + i128::from(self.offset_ms);
        at.clamp(i128::from(i64::MIN), i128::from(i64::MAX)) as i64
    }

    /// `every 1 month at day 1 09:00 UTC`, `every 1d at 03:00 UTC`, `every 2m`.
    pub fn describe(&self) -> String {
        let clock = |ms: u64| format!("{:02}:{:02}", ms / 3_600_000, ms % 3_600_000 / 60_000);
        if self.months > 0 {
            let (n, unit) = if self.months % 12 == 0 { (self.months / 12, "year") } else { (self.months, "month") };
            let day = self.offset_ms / 86_400_000 + 1;
            return format!("every {n} {unit}{} at day {day} {} UTC", if n == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, clock(self.offset_ms % 86_400_000));
        }
        let every = self.every_ms;
        let unit = [(86_400_000, "d"), (3_600_000, "h"), (60_000, "m"), (1_000, "s"), (1, "ms")].into_iter().find(|(n, _)| every % n == 0).unwrap_or((1, "ms"));
        let at = if self.offset_ms > 0 { format!(" at {} UTC", clock(self.offset_ms)) } else { String::new() };
        format!("every {}{}{at}", every / unit.0, unit.1)
    }

    /// The period and offset fit one of the two shapes.
    pub fn valid(&self) -> bool {
        if self.months > 0 {
            return self.every_ms == 0 && self.months <= CALENDAR_MONTHS_LIMIT && self.offset_ms < CALENDAR_OFFSET_LIMIT_MS;
        }
        self.every_ms != 0 && self.every_ms <= i64::MAX as u64 && self.offset_ms < self.every_ms
    }
}
//! Development time drives the actual HTTP host and both device stores without sleeping through a period.
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use snapback4_device::{call::call, device::{Backend, Device}, memory::MemoryStore, store::DeviceStore};
use std::{fs, io::{BufRead, BufReader, Read, Write}, net::TcpStream, path::PathBuf, process::{Child, Command, Output, Stdio}, time::{Duration, Instant}};

const BIN: &str = env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4");
const SOURCE: &str = "use personas alice\ntable stories:\n  at: time\n  expiresAt: time ?\n  by byExpiry: expiresAt\n  expire at .expiresAt\n  public 'clock test'\n  insert <- allow\n  sync public\ntable counters:\n  n: int\n  at: time\n  public 'clock test'\n  insert <- allow\n  sync public\nmutation post():\n  return insert stories {at: now, expiresAt: now + 180000}\nmutation counter():\n  return insert counters {n: 0, at: now}\nquery stories():\n  return stories first 10 by byId\nquery counters():\n  return counters first 10 by byId\nquery time():\n  return now\njob tick():\n  chunk c in counters first 10 by byId:\n    update counters[c.id] {n: c.n + 1, at: now}\nschedule ticking every 2m: tick\n";

struct Fixture(PathBuf);

#[test]
fn maintained_value_rule_keeps_permit_deadline_over_http_and_two_read_through_links() {
    let f=Fixture::new("count-deadline");
    let schema=include_str!("../../snapback4-core/tests/row_view_witness/schema.q")
        .replace("MEMBER_EXPIRY", "").replace("  public", "  public\n  insert <- allow")
        .replace("PREDICATE","not exists unread[member = .member, value = 0] or exists permits[member = .member, slot = 0] or exists permits[member = .member, slot = 1]");
    let source=format!("use personas alice\n{schema}\nmutation setup():\n  insert rooms {{id:'r'}}\n  insert members {{id:'u',room:'r'}}\n  insert messages {{id:'m',room:'r',until:now+10000}}\n  insert permits {{id:'p',member:'u',until:now+20000}}\n  insert gates {{id:'g',member:'u'}}\n  insert links {{id:'l',gate:'g'}}\n  insert leaves {{id:'z',link:'l'}}\n  return now+20000\n")
        .replace("table gates:\n", "table gates:\n  insert <- allow\n")
        .replace("table links:\n", "table links:\n  insert <- allow\n")
        .replace("table leaves:\n", "table leaves:\n  insert <- allow\n");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),source).unwrap();
    let host=f.start(&["dev","--no-watch","--hold-schedules"]);
    let setup=host.post("/m/setup",json!({"id":"seed","args":{}}));
    assert_eq!(setup["state"],"sent","{setup}");
    let deadline=setup["result"].clone();assert!(deadline.is_i64(),"{setup}");
    for advanced in [false,true] {
        if advanced {host.post("/dev/clock/advance",json!({"duration":"11s"}));}
        for name in ["direct","linked","leaf"] {
            let r=host.post(&format!("/q/{name}"),json!({"args":{}}));
            assert_eq!(r["complete"],true,"{r}");assert!(!r["data"].is_null(),"{r}");
            assert_eq!(r["nextExpiry"],deadline,"{name} advanced={advanced}: {r}");
        }
    }
    host.post("/dev/clock/advance",json!({"duration":"10s"}));
    for name in ["direct","linked","leaf"] {assert!(host.query(name).is_null());}
}

#[test]
fn doctor_reports_the_exact_schedule_period_without_minute_truncation() {
    for (period, shown) in [("90s", "90s"), ("90001ms", "90001ms"), ("120s", "2m")] {
        let f = Fixture::new(&format!("period-{period}"));
        fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), SOURCE.replace("every 2m:", &format!("every {period}:"))).unwrap();
        let _host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules"]);
        let output = f.command(&["doctor"]);
        let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        assert!(output.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr));
        assert!(text.contains(&format!("schedule  ticking every {shown} → tick")), "{text}");
    }
}

impl Fixture {
    fn new(label: &str) -> Self {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-clock-{}-{label}", std::process::id()));
        fs::create_dir_all(path.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        fs::write(path.join("snapback/schema.q"), SOURCE).unwrap(); Self(path)
    }
    fn command(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output { Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&self.0).args(args).output().unwrap() }
    fn start(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Host { self.start_on_port(args,"0") }
    fn start_on_port(&self, args: &[&str], port: &str) -> Host {
        let mut child = Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&self.0).args(args).args(["--port", port]).stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(Stdio::inherit()).spawn().unwrap();
        let mut line = String::new(); BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().unwrap()).read_line(&mut line).unwrap();
        let host = Host { child, address: line.trim().trim_start_matches("http://").into() };
        assert!(!host.address.is_empty(), "host did not start"); host
    }
}
impl Drop for Fixture { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0); } }
struct Host { child: Child, address: String }
impl Drop for Host { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = self.child.kill(); let _ = self.child.wait(); } }
impl Host {
    fn request(&self, method: &str, path: &str, body: Value) -> Value {
        self.auth_request(method, path, body, "x-snapback-persona: alice")
    }
    fn auth_request(&self, method: &str, path: &str, body: Value, auth: &str) -> Value {
        let body = body.to_string();
        let device = if path == "/sync" { format!("x-snapback-device: {}\r\n", serde_json::to_string(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine::current()).unwrap()) } else { String::new() };
        let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(&self.address).unwrap(); stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(30))).unwrap();
        write!(stream, "{method} {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\n{auth}\r\n{device}Content-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len()).unwrap();
        let mut answer = String::new(); stream.read_to_string(&mut answer).unwrap();
        serde_json::from_str(answer.split_once("\r\n\r\n").unwrap().1).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}: {answer}"))
    }
    fn post(&self, path: &str, body: Value) -> Value { self.request("POST", path, body) }
    fn query(&self, name: &str) -> Value { self.post(&format!("/q/{name}"), json!({"args":{}}))["data"].clone() }
    fn raw(&self, method: &str, path: &str, body: &str) -> String {
        let device = if path == "/sync" { format!("x-snapback-device: {}\r\n", serde_json::to_string(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine::current()).unwrap()) } else { String::new() };
        let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(&self.address).unwrap();
        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(30))).unwrap();
        write!(stream, "{method} {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\n{device}Content-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len()).unwrap();
        let mut answer = String::new();
        stream.read_to_string(&mut answer).unwrap();
        answer
    }
}

fn device_sync<S: DeviceStore>(mut device: Device<S>, snapshot: &Value, stream: &Value, local_now: i64) {
    let applied = call(&mut device, json!({"op":"apply", "page":snapshot, "first":true}));
    assert!(applied.get("denied").is_none(), "{applied}");
    let read = |device: &mut Device<S>, name| call(device, json!({"op":"query", "name":name, "viewer":"dev:alice", "args":{}, "now":local_now}));
    assert_eq!(read(&mut device, "stories")["ok"]["data"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    let applied = call(&mut device, json!({"op":"apply", "page":stream, "first":false}));
    assert!(applied.get("denied").is_none(), "{applied}");
    assert_eq!(read(&mut device, "stories")["ok"]["data"], json!([]), "deletion must sync even with the device clock unchanged");
    assert_eq!(read(&mut device, "counters")["ok"]["data"][0]["n"], 1);
}

#[test]
fn advancing_clock_expires_rows_and_fires_two_minute_schedule_on_server_and_both_stores() {
    let f = Fixture::new("sync");
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch"]);
    let sent = host.post("/m/post", json!({"id":"story", "args":{}})); assert_eq!(sent["state"], "sent", "{sent}");
    let sent = host.post("/m/counter", json!({"id":"counter", "args":{}})); assert_eq!(sent["state"], "sent", "{sent}");
    let local_now = host.query("time").as_i64().unwrap();
    let snapshot = host.post("/sync", json!({"from":0}));
    assert_eq!(host.query("stories").as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(host.query("counters")[0]["n"], 0);
    let start = Instant::now();
    let advanced = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"185s"}));
    assert!(advanced.get("denied").is_none(), "{advanced}");
    assert_eq!(host.query("stories"), json!([]), "expiry currently needs a real 185-second wait");
    assert_eq!(host.query("counters")[0]["n"], 1, "every 2m fires once after missed boundaries");
    let stream = host.post("/sync", json!({"from":snapshot["watermark"], "stream":true}));
    assert!(!stream["events"].as_array().unwrap().is_empty(), "{stream}");
    let backend: Backend = serde_json::from_value(host.request("GET", "/schema", json!(null))).unwrap();
    device_sync(Device::open_store(MemoryStore::new(), Some(backend.clone())).unwrap(), &snapshot, &stream, local_now);
    device_sync(Device::open(&f.0.join("device.sqlite"), Some(backend)).unwrap(), &snapshot, &stream, local_now);
    println!("clock: advanced_ms=185000 expiry_rows=1 schedule_runs=1 device_stores=2 wall_ms={}", start.elapsed().as_millis());
}

fn output_json(out: Output) -> Value {
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    serde_json::from_slice(&out.stdout).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn clock_advance_expires_presence_grace_and_a_new_poll_reconnects() {
    let f = Fixture::new("presence");
    fs::write(f.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("{SOURCE}\nquery connections():\n  return connected[viewer]\n")).unwrap();
    let host = f.start(&["dev", "--no-watch", "--hold-schedules", "--clock-offset", "+25h"]);
    assert_eq!(host.query("connections"), 0);
    host.request("GET", "/changes?wait=0", json!(null));
    assert_eq!(host.query("connections"), 1, "an answered poll has grace");
    let start = Instant::now();
    let port = host.address.rsplit(':').next().unwrap();
    let advanced = output_json(f.command(&["clock", "advance", "36s", "--port", port]));
    assert_eq!(advanced["advancedMs"], 36_000);
    assert_eq!(host.query("connections"), 0, "development time must expire an ended poll's grace");
    host.request("GET", "/changes?wait=0", json!(null));
    assert_eq!(host.query("connections"), 1, "a new poll reconnects at the advanced time");
    let advanced = host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"36s"}));
    assert_eq!(advanced["advancedMs"], 36_000);
    assert_eq!(host.query("connections"), 0);
    println!("presence: advances=2 advanced_ms=72000 grace_ms=35000 wall_ms={}", start.elapsed().as_millis());
    let seq = host.request("GET", "/probe", json!(null))["seq"].as_u64().unwrap();
    let mut poll = TcpStream::connect(&host.address).unwrap();
    poll.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10))).unwrap();
    write!(poll, "GET /changes?since={seq}&wait=25&heartbeat=1 HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
    let mut poll = BufReader::new(poll);
    let mut header = String::new(); poll.read_line(&mut header).unwrap();
    assert!(header.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200"), "{header}");
    // Receiving the streaming header proves the poll entered, without a sleep.
    for _ in 0..2 {
        assert_eq!(host.post("/dev/clock/advance", json!({"duration":"36s"}))["advancedMs"], 36_000);
        assert_eq!(host.query("connections"), 1, "an open poll survives clock advances");
    }
}

//! A process-local development clock. The release host always reads wall time.
use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

const MAX_TIME: i64 = snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX;

pub fn input(message: impl Into<String>) -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INPUT", "clock", message).guide("clock") }
pub fn release() -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY", "clock", "clock controls require dev; serve always uses wall time").guide("clock") }

/// Isolated time tests stay inside an assigned range, or request a free port.
pub(crate) fn isolated_command() -> String {
    isolated_range(std::env::var("PORT_LO").ok().as_deref(), std::env::var("PORT_HI").ok().as_deref())
}
fn isolated_range(lo: Option<&str>, hi: Option<&str>) -> String {
    match (lo.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok()), hi.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())) {
        (Some(lo), Some(hi)) if lo > 0 && hi >= lo => format!("dev --memory --port {lo}-{hi} within the current range"),
        _ => "dev --memory --port 0".into(),
    }
}

pub fn duration(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    if text.starts_with('-') { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
    let text = text.strip_prefix('+').unwrap_or(text);
    let split = text.bytes().position(|b| !b.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(text.len());
    let (number, unit) = text.split_at(split);
    let multiplier = match unit { "ms" => 1, "s" => 1_000, "m" => 60_000, "h" => 3_600_000, "d" => 86_400_000, "w" => 604_800_000, _ => 0 };
    number.parse::<i64>().ok().and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(multiplier)).filter(|n| *n > 0 && *n <= MAX_TIME)
        .ok_or_else(|| input("expected a positive whole duration with ms, s, m, h, d or w, such as +25h"))
}

pub fn offset(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    if let Some(rest) = text.strip_prefix('-') { duration(rest).map(|n| -n) } else { duration(text) }
}

fn time(value: Option<i64>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    value.filter(|n| (0..=MAX_TIME).contains(n)).ok_or_else(|| input("time must be Unix milliseconds in 0..9007199254740991; choose a smaller time or duration"))
}

pub fn run_now(text: Option<&String>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    let now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    match text {
        None => Ok(now),
        Some(text) if text.starts_with('+') => time(now.checked_add(duration(text)?)),
        Some(text) => time(text.parse().ok()),
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Clock {
    dev: bool,
    base: i64,
    started: Instant,
    epoch: String,
    revision: u64,
    #[cfg(test)]
    frozen: bool,
}

impl Clock {
    pub fn new(dev: bool, offset: i64) -> Result<Self, Refusal> {
        if !dev && offset != 0 { return Err(release()); }
        Ok(Self { dev, base: time(snapback4_core::now_ms().checked_add(offset))?, started: Instant::now(),
            epoch: snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?, revision: 0, #[cfg(test)] frozen: false })
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn freeze(&mut self, now: i64) { self.base = now; self.frozen = true; }
    pub fn now(&self) -> i64 {
        #[cfg(test)] if self.frozen { return self.base; }
        if !self.dev { return snapback4_core::now_ms(); }
        self.base.saturating_add(self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64).min(MAX_TIME)
    }
    /// Identifies samples taken before/after a restart or deliberate clock jump.
    pub fn revision(&self) -> String { format!("{}:{}", self.epoch, self.revision) }
    pub fn presence_now(&self) -> i64 {
        // Production grace is elapsed real time, unaffected by wall-clock corrections.
        if self.dev { self.now() } else { self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64 }
    }
    /// Back to wall time: the offset `--clock-offset` and every advance added
    /// is dropped (a development reset's `{"clock":"reset"}`).
    pub fn reset(&mut self) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
        self.revision += 1;
        self.base = snapback4_core::now_ms();
        self.started = Instant::now();
        #[cfg(test)] { self.frozen = false; }
        Ok(self.base)
    }
    pub fn advance(&mut self, milliseconds: i64) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
        if milliseconds <= 0 { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
        let next = time(self.now().checked_add(milliseconds))?;
        self.revision += 1;
        self.base = next;
        self.started = Instant::now();
        Ok(next)
    }
}

/// The control CLI only talks to a loopback dev host. It does not open its store.
pub fn command(options: &crate::verbs::Options) -> i32 {
    let result = (|| -> Result<String, String> {
        if options.flags.contains_key("release") { return Err(release().to_string()); }
        if options.positionals.len() != 2 || options.positionals[0] != "advance" {
            return Err(input(format!("usage: {}", crate::cli_flags::synopsis("clock"))).to_string());
        }
        duration(&options.positionals[1]).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let (address, instance) = crate::verbs::dev_address(options)?;
        let body = serde_json::json!({"duration":options.positionals[1],"eachBoundary":options.flags.contains_key("each-boundary")}).to_string();
        // The common HTTP/1.0 exchange is EOF-delimited and bounds replies at
        // 64 MiB, enough for the preflight's complete explicit window report.
        let (status, json) = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_dev(address, "/dev/clock/advance", instance.as_deref(), &body, Duration::from_secs(60)).map_err(|error| {
            let evidence = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange(address, "GET", "/dev/status", None, "", Duration::from_secs(5)).ok()
                .map(|(_, status)| format!("now={}, skewMs={}", status["now"], status["skewMs"]))
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "clock status unavailable".into());
            format!("clock advance reply failed at {address}: {error}; time may already have advanced ({evidence}); inspect /dev/status and queued work before retrying; for an isolated dev pass --port N")
        })?;
        if let Some(denied) = json.get("denied") {
            let clock = json["message"].as_str().map(|message| format!("; time remains advanced: {message}")).unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(format!("{}: {}{clock}\n  guide: clock", denied["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("E_CLOCK_INPUT"), denied["message"].as_str().unwrap_or("clock advance failed")));
        }
        if status != 200 { return Err(format!("dev refused clock advance: {json}")); }
        Ok(json.to_string())
    })();
    match result { Ok(body) => { println!("{body}"); 0 }, Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); 1 } }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod isolated_tests {
    #[test]
    fn time_test_guidance_uses_only_valid_assigned_ranges_or_a_free_port() {
        assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(Some("24000"), Some("24009")), "dev --memory --port 24000-24009 within the current range");
        for (lo,hi) in [(None,None),(Some("0"),Some("3")),(Some("4"),Some("3")),(Some("bad"),Some("3"))] {
            assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(lo,hi), "dev --memory --port 0");
        }
    }
}
    /// fired for is committed with the job row, so a restart never fires
    /// twice for one period; a schedule seen for the first time fires from
    /// its next boundary. Returns the names fired.
    pub fn fire_schedules(&mut self, now: i64) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, 0..self.schema.schedules.len(), None)
    }

    /// One atomic schedule boundary, so the host can release its writer between
    /// schedules. A changed/shortened schema is revisited on the next tick.
    pub fn fire_schedule_at(&mut self, now: i64, index: usize) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, index..index.saturating_add(1), None)
    }

    /// Admit one historical period on the current clock. In particular an
    /// action's deadline begins at admission, not at the historical boundary.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.1 — period identity and admission time are distinct during explicit catch-up.
    pub fn fire_schedule_boundary_at(&mut self, now: i64, index: usize, boundary: i64) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        let schedule = self.schema.schedules.get(index).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("unknown schedule index".into()))?;
        if schedule.boundary(boundary) != boundary || boundary > schedule.boundary(now) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Input("schedule window must end at an elapsed boundary".into()));
        }
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, index..index.saturating_add(1), Some(boundary))
    }

    fn fire_schedule_range(&mut self, now: i64, range: std::ops::Range<usize>, selected: Option<i64>) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        let mut fired = Vec::new();
        for schedule in self.schema.schedules.get(range).unwrap_or(&[]).to_vec() {
            let boundary = selected.unwrap_or_else(|| schedule.boundary(now));
            let key = format!("schedule:{}", schedule.name);
            let shape_key = format!("schedule-shape:{}", schedule.name);
            let shape = digest(&schedule);
            let program = self.programs.get(&schedule.target).filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
                .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore its target before firing it", schedule.name, schedule.target)))?.clone();
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action && !schedule.service.as_deref().is_some_and(crate::identity::is_service) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into()));
            }
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job && schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a scheduled job runs as the system, not a service seat".into()));
            }
            let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
            let previous_shape: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [&shape_key], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            if previous_shape.as_ref().is_some_and(|previous| previous != &shape) {
                tx.set_meta(&key, &boundary.to_string())?;
                tx.set_meta(&shape_key, &shape)?;
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            }
            tx.set_meta(&shape_key, &shape)?;
            let last: Option<i64> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [&key], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.and_then(|value| value.parse().ok());
            if last.is_some_and(|last| last >= boundary) {
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            }
            let Some(_) = last else {
                tx.set_meta(&key, &boundary.to_string())?;
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            };
            let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
            if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") {
                args.insert("periodStart".to_owned(), Value::Int(schedule.previous(boundary)));
            }
            if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") {
                args.insert("periodEnd".to_owned(), Value::Int(boundary));
            }
            let id = crate::ids::mint(now, &mut self.minter)?;
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let principal = schedule.service.as_deref().expect("checked service seat");
                validate_inputs(&self.schema, &program, &mut args)?;
                super::actions::authorize(&program, principal)?;
                let raw = serde_json::Value::Object(crate::wire::map_to_json(&args));
                let context = Context::new(principal, args).at(now);
                // @ref LLP 3000.009#5.2 — the boundary and service-seat admission are one commit.
                super::actions::insert_request(&tx, &self.schema, &program, &id, &context, &raw, true, now)?;
            } else {
                #[cfg(feature = "native")]
                let program_hash = job_admission_hash(&program, self.native.as_deref().map(super::native::Module::digest));
                #[cfg(not(feature = "native"))]
                let program_hash = job_admission_hash(&program, None);
                insert_job(tx.conn(), &id, &schedule.target, &args, &program_hash, now)?;
            }
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key, value) VALUES (?1, ?2) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value", params![key, boundary.to_string()])?;
            tx.commit()?;
            fired.push(schedule.name.clone());
        }
        Ok(fired)
    }
}

/// One chunk of a native job (LLP 3000.005): the module runs once with
/// the state it returned last time, inside one transaction under the job
/// ceilings; its rows and events commit with the state, a refusal parks
/// it, a trap parks it with the reason. Over the engine's fields, not the
/// engine, because the transaction borrows its store.
#[cfg(feature = "native")]
fn native_chunk(tx: crate::store::Tx<'_>, schema: &crate::schema::Schema, module: Option<&super::native::Module>, logs: &mut super::native::Logs, minter: crate::ids::Mint, head: Head, program: &crate::ir::Program, now: i64, dev: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    use super::native::{self, Step};
    tx.savepoint("job_chunk")?;
    let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
    let mut context = Context::system().at(now).with_minter(minter);
    context.args = head.args.clone();

# LLP 3000: Snapback 4 — the decided-substrates candidate

**Type:** Explainer
**Status:** Active
**Role:** Root of the Snapback 4 corpus (LLPs 3000 and up)
**Systems:** Everything under `snapback4/` once it exists; the bakeoff protocol shared with Snapback 3 (LLP 2000) and Snapback 2 (LLP 1000)
**Author:** Charlie Cheever / Claude Fable 5.1
**Date:** 2026-09-12
**Revised:** 2026-10-13 (FLAKE: isolate real-timer client fixtures and keep quote layout pooled); 2026-10-13 (§7: timed-out compiler-group inspector retries within the unchanged drain deadline); 2026-10-12 (§7: a reference declares what deleting its row does, LLP 3000.039);
